# Beacon UX/Flow/Product Teardown — 537 findings (2026-06-15)

> Generated by a 20-persona + 14-dimension multi-agent teardown (workflow wf_a189140d-085, 24 agents). Each finding is grounded in a real file or a concrete ABSENCE. Personas: a user automating SEO/AEO for a Wix site with SEMrush/Profound/Wix/GSC/GA4/Clarity, wanting set-and-forget for 6 months.

**Severity:** ⛔ 147 horrific · ⚠️ 252 bad · ▫︎ 138 annoying

> **Verification pass (2026-06-15, code-grounded):** This worklist was generated by agents reading a stale product map. Verified against current code, the bulk of the worst-first clusters are **already fixed** by prior batches (golden-path, honest-copy, a11y commit `1ca76ff`). Confirmed already-fixed (spot-checked in code): the entire "automation lies / phantom-cron" copy cluster — #2, #6, #9, #18, #28, #31, #33, #38, #39, #40, #43, #44, #45, #52, #55, #56, #62, #63, #77, #78, #97, #98, #99, #100, #109 (all now on-demand "refresh your connected data" copy); the launch `/today` 404 — #117, #118, #121, #133 (all redirects now `to: "/"`); first-run CTA — #14, #44, #48, #68 (FirstReadingWaiting has a "Connect your data sources" CTA + honest `nextReadingDescription` = "once you refresh your connected data"); connect/disconnect banner a11y — #166 (`status-safe`→`status-success`), #167 (`role`/`aria-live`); onboarding stepper a11y — #147 (`aria-current`+visible "Step N of 4"); chart `role="img"` — #366; metric toggle ARIA — #398; health-strip ARIA — #399; signup resend/spam-hint — #129; local empty-state — #145; competitors-step honest "optional" framing — #103, #135 (partial); jargon relabel — #282/#323 (`edit_h1`→"Page headline", `edit_meta`→"Search result preview", `add_schema`→"Search-engine details (schema)" already in ACTION_ROW_TYPE_LABEL). **Newly fixed this pass:** #168, #195 (Yelp residual), #367 (see inline ✅). **Attended / left for a product or entangled decision:** the connectors-client per-card "Sync now" wiring cluster (#21, #49, #91, #111, #149–#157, #162, #165) — the Today "Refresh my data" button already covers refresh on the Today surface, and per-card wiring + the legacy/v2 dual-surface + connectors-nav discoverability (#26, #115, #151, #154, #155, #157, #169) are larger entangled changes; scheduler/autopilot findings (#1, #3, #4, #7, #12, #15, #22, #24, #25, #57, #79, #82) are pivot-inherent (crons abandoned by design — honest fix is on-demand copy + Refresh, already shipped).


## Autopilot / automation gap (set-and-forget that isn't) (114)

**1. ⛔ There is NO autopilot — and the only 'Refresh / Run reading' buttons are on an operator-only page that 404s for me**
- *Surface:* /diagnostics/connectors + whole product (autopilot promise)
- *Why it's bad:* I signed up to set-and-forget this for 6 months. But this file confirms crons are off ('now that nightly crons are off', line 205) and the ONLY way to pull fresh data or run an AI reading is the 'Refresh all connected sources' / 'Run ChatGPT reading' / 'Recompute proof' buttons here — on a page guarded by isOperatorModeServer() (line 70-72) that gives me a 404. So my data NEVER updates on its own AND I can't update it manually either. The product is dead the moment I close the tab. I'm paying for a robot that does nothing while I sleep.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Either turn the nightly refresh/reading crons back on for paying tenants, OR expose a customer-safe 'Refresh my data now' + 'Check AI answers now' button on / or /settings/connectors. Set-and-forget customers need at least one of those two to exist.

**2. ⛔ Copy all over the app promises 'every morning', 'tomorrow morning', 'next daily reading' — but nothing ever runs**
- *Surface:* /today + /onboard/review + /prompts + multiple cards
- *Why it's bad:* Line 268 literally tells me 'Beacon checks AI visibility multiple times each morning; backup attempts run automatically.' first-reading-waiting.tsx says 'Your first dashboard will appear after the next daily reading.' today-client.tsx line 602-605 says 'check back here tomorrow morning.' prompts/page.tsx line 158 says 'Beacon checks AI visibility every morning.' With crons OFF (confirmed in diagnostics/connectors), these are flat-out lies. I'll wait days for a 'tomorrow' that never comes, decide the product is broken, and churn — while believing it's my fault for not waiting long enough.
- *Grounding:* src/components/today/data-freshness-heartbeat.tsx
- *Fix:* Global copy sweep: replace every 'tomorrow morning / every morning / next daily reading / backup attempts run automatically' string with the truth ('Click Refresh to pull your latest data'). Promising automation you don't deliver is the fastest way to lose a non-technical owner's trust.

**3. ⛔ Nothing in the product lets me schedule or automate anything — the core thing I bought doesn't exist as a feature**
- *Surface:* Onboarding → first value (overall expectation gap)
- *Why it's bad:* I bought this to run itself for 6 months. I searched the nav, settings tabs (Import/Config/Prompts/Data), and connectors UI — there is no 'run automatically', no schedule, no 'check my site weekly' anywhere a customer can reach. The only automation (GitHub workflow crons) is off and invisible to me. A set-and-forget SEO tool with zero customer-facing automation control is selling a promise the UI can't keep.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no customer-facing scheduling/automation UI anywhere (no cron toggle, no 'run nightly' setting in src/lib/navigation.ts, settings tabs, or connectors UI)
- *Fix:* Ship a simple customer setting: 'How often should Beacon check your site and AI answers? (Daily / Weekly)' that actually drives a real scheduled job. Until automation exists, stop marketing set-and-forget.

**4. ⛔ There is NO autopilot — fresh data and new recs require manual clicks on a page customers can't even reach**
- *Surface:* Whole app / data freshness
- *Why it's bad:* I want to set this up once and let it run for 6 months across 12 sites. Instead the ENTIRE architecture is crons-off/on-demand: the only place to 'Refresh all connected sources' and 'Run today's AI reading' is /diagnostics/connectors (page.tsx line 70: `if (!isOperatorModeServer()) notFound()`) — a 404 for a normal account. So a paying agency has zero way to refresh data or generate recs from the customer UI, and zero scheduling. The product's core promise to me is structurally absent.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Surface a customer-facing 'Refresh all sources' + 'Run AI reading' control on /settings/connectors (not just operator diagnostics), and add real per-tenant scheduled refresh so it runs unattended.

**5. ⛔ Approved edits publish one edit per manual click — no bulk push, no scheduling, capped at 10/day/site**
- *Surface:* Wix publishing
- *Why it's bad:* approveAndPushRecommendedEdit(args:{editId}) pushes exactly ONE edit and runs a live probe per call (recommendations/actions.ts line 820); there is no batch-push action anywhere. On top of that MAX_PUSHES_PER_DAY = 10 per property (push/caps.ts line 22). So to ship a backlog across 12 sites I must hand-click each edit, and I'm throttled to 10/site/day with no way to queue or schedule the rest. 'Set and forget for 6 months' is impossible — publishing is fully manual, one-by-one, and rate-limited.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/recommendations/actions.ts
- *Fix:* Add a bulk 'Approve & Push selected' action and an optional auto-publish/scheduled-publish mode (respecting caps) so an agency can ship a reviewed batch without 100+ individual clicks.

**6. ⛔ Freshness banner claims automatic 'multiple checks each morning' and 'backup attempts run automatically' — crons are dead**
- *Surface:* / (Today, V2 + legacy data-freshness-heartbeat)
- *Why it's bad:* I connected six tools expecting set-and-forget for 6 months. The freshness heartbeat literally tells me 'Beacon checks AI visibility multiple times each morning; backup attempts run automatically' and 'Backup AI checks run automatically. If data stays stale through the morning, today's check didn't complete.' Every one of those GitHub Actions crons was REMOVED ('2026-06-15: nightly cron REMOVED — the operator disabled ALL GitHub Actions'). So the product is actively lying to me about its own provenance: nothing refreshes unless I manually click. As a data skeptic this is the single most disqualifying thing a tool can do — fabricate its own data lineage.
- *Grounding:* src/components/today/data-freshness-heartbeat.tsx
- *Fix:* Rip out the 'multiple times each morning / backup attempts run automatically' copy in sublineFor() (data-freshness-heartbeat.tsx:268,273,277). With crons off the only honest copy is 'Beacon refreshes when you run a reading — last reading {rel}. Run a reading to update.' Drive band/headline from actual last-run timestamps, never from an assumed schedule.

**7. ⛔ There is NO autopilot — every cron was deleted; the product I 'set and forget' literally never runs again until I manually click a button**
- *Surface:* automation model / vercel.json + .github/workflows
- *Why it's bad:* I bought 6 months of hands-off automation. vercel.json has `"crons": []` and ALL FIVE GitHub workflows (daily-native-poll, daily-scan, nightly-generation, morning-digest, poll-canary) had their `schedule:` blocks removed and replaced with `workflow_dispatch:` only — daily-native-poll.yml line 90 literally says 'nightly cron REMOVED — the operator disabled ALL GitHub Actions.' So after I close the tab, nothing polls AI, nothing scans my site, nothing regenerates recs, nothing emails me. My data freezes on day 1 and I'd never know. This is the single biggest lie in the product for me.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/vercel.json
- *Fix:* Either (a) restore at least the daily-native-poll + nightly-generation `schedule:` crons (or a Vercel cron) so connected tenants actually get nightly data as sold, or (b) STOP every UI string that promises scheduling and tell me plainly: 'Beacon only updates when you click Refresh — there is no background automation.' Pick one; today the code says (a) is gone but the UI still promises it.

**8. ⛔ The ONLY 'Refresh all sources' and 'Run today's AI reading' buttons are operator-gated — as a paying customer I can never reach them**
- *Surface:* /diagnostics/connectors (the only refresh cockpit)
- *Why it's bad:* diagnostics/connectors/actions.ts gates refreshAllDataSources, runTodaysReadingForPlatform AND recomputeProof behind `isOperatorModeServer()` (lines 123, 267, 349 all return `not_operator`). The page itself 404s for non-operators (page.tsx line 70-72). With crons off, these buttons are the ONLY way to pull fresh data — and they're invisible to me. So a normal customer has literally zero in-product way to refresh anything. My dashboard is frozen forever and the product can't even be operated by the person who paid for it.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/actions.ts
- *Fix:* Expose a customer-safe 'Refresh my data' + 'Run today's AI reading' control on a real customer route (e.g. /settings/connectors) gated by tenant ownership, not the global operator env flag. The refresh actions already exist — they're just locked in the operator-only attic.

**9. ⛔ The dashboard tells me 'Next reading scheduled — runs in scheduled attempts throughout the morning' when NOTHING is scheduled**
- *Surface:* / (Today dashboard) — poll-health-calm-banner
- *Why it's bad:* poll-health-calm-banner.tsx line 41-48 renders 'Next reading scheduled' and 'Beacon's daily AI reading runs in scheduled attempts throughout the morning.' This is flatly false — daily-native-poll.yml has no schedule, it's workflow_dispatch-only. So Beacon sits there reassuring me a reading is coming 'this morning' while in reality it will never run unless someone manually fires it. I'd go on vacation trusting this banner and come back to a month of stale data.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/components/today/poll-health-calm-banner.tsx
- *Fix:* Delete/rewrite this banner. If there genuinely is no schedule, say 'No automatic reading is scheduled — click Refresh to update' with a working button. Never claim a scheduled run exists when vercel.json crons is empty and the workflow is dispatch-only.

**10. ⛔ Empty recs say 'The queue regenerates nightly... Come back tomorrow' — it does not regenerate, ever**
- *Surface:* /recommendations (empty state)
- *Why it's bad:* recommendations-client.tsx line 1757-1759: 'The queue regenerates nightly from the latest prompt observations. Come back tomorrow.' The nightly-generation workflow's schedule was removed (nightly-generation.yml line 45: 'nightly cron removed'). So I come back tomorrow, and the next day, and the next — the queue is identical because nothing regenerated it. This is the exact set-and-forget promise I paid for, told to my face, and it's a lie baked into the empty state.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/recommendations/recommendations-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Replace with the truth: 'New recommendations appear after you refresh your connected data (Settings → Connectors → Sync now).' Link to a working refresh control.

**11. ⛔ My very first screen says 'Beacon is preparing your first reading... appears after the next daily reading' — but no daily reading is scheduled, so the first reading never comes**
- *Surface:* first-reading-waiting (brand-new tenant landing)
- *Why it's bad:* first-reading-waiting.tsx line 39-46: 'Beacon is preparing your first reading. Your first dashboard will appear after the next daily reading.' For a fresh tenant with crons off, there IS no next daily reading — it waits forever. The two links it offers ('Review tracked prompts', 'Recommendations after first reading') don't trigger a reading either. A new customer literally cannot get past this waiting screen without finding the hidden operator-only refresh page.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/components/today/first-reading-waiting.tsx
- *Fix:* Put a 'Run my first reading now' button on this screen wired to the (customer-exposed) runTodaysReading action, so the first reading actually happens when I click. Don't promise a 'next daily reading' that's disabled.

**12. ⛔ There is no away-mode, no 'run daily' toggle, no schedule setting anywhere in the customer app**
- *Surface:* automation model — ABSENCE of any scheduling UI
- *Why it's bad:* I grepped all of src/app + src/components for run-daily / schedule-sync / auto-run / away-mode / vacation toggles: the only hits are methodology FAQ lines saying 'no automatic syncing.' For a buyer whose entire value prop is 'set it and forget it for 6 months,' the absence of a single 'keep my data fresh automatically' control is the product failing its core promise. Profound (what I'm replacing) just ran. Beacon makes me a manual button-pusher.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no scheduling/away-mode/auto-run toggle exists anywhere under src/app or src/components (only 'no automatic syncing' disclaimers)
- *Fix:* Ship a real scheduling control: a per-tenant 'Auto-refresh daily' toggle that re-enables the poll/generation crons for that tenant (the engines are budget-guarded and idempotent already). This is THE feature that makes Beacon set-and-forget.

**13. ⛔ Wix publishing lives ONLY on an operator-gated page I can't open — so even manual publishing is impossible for me**
- *Surface:* Wix auto-push — /diagnostics/wix
- *Why it's bad:* diagnostics/wix/page.tsx is the only place 'Approve & Push' exists (the recommendations-client has no Push button at all). It's gated by canPublishForCurrentTenant() and 404s otherwise (line 38). The connectors-client lets me CONNECT Wix and even reassures me 'Approved edits can now publish to your site' (line 411) — but there is no customer-facing surface to actually approve+push a single edit. I connected Wix expecting auto-publish and I can't even publish manually.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/wix/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Surface Approve & Push on the customer /recommendations detail page (gated by tenant publish auth), not buried in /diagnostics/wix. The connect success message must not promise publishing the UI doesn't expose.

**14. ⛔ Tells me to wait for 'the next daily reading' that never comes (crons are off) and points me to a Settings page that has no connectors**
- *Surface:* first-reading screen — src/components/today/first-reading-waiting.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* After launch I land here. It says 'Your first dashboard will appear after the next daily reading' (line 43) and 'adjust any of this later in Settings' (line 113) — but crons are OFF, so no daily reading ever fires, and Settings has no Connectors tab. The two links offered are only /prompts and /recommendations (lines 138-149), neither of which connects my data sources. I'm a mobile shop owner left staring at a permanent waiting screen with no way to make anything happen.
- *Grounding:* src/components/today/first-reading-waiting.tsx
- *Fix:* Replace the passive 'daily reading' promise with an explicit, mobile-friendly 'Connect your tools and run your first reading' button that links to connectors and triggers an on-demand pull. Don't promise an automatic reading that the crons-off architecture can't deliver.

**15. ⛔ There is no autopilot and no way to schedule a refresh — I expected set-and-forget for 6 months**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors — connectors-client.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* I connected all six tools assuming it would run itself. But the sync server actions (syncGscNow etc.) aren't even wired to buttons in connectors-client.tsx, and the only GSC sync UI is gated behind `GBP_AFFORDANCES_ENABLED = false` (line 43). There is no schedule, no auto-run, nothing. To get fresh data I'd have to manually click refresh on an operator diagnostics page I can't even reach from the nav. On a phone, between customers, manually re-pulling six connectors daily for six months is a fantasy. This is the single biggest gap between what I was sold and what exists.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Build a real scheduled/auto-refresh path (or at minimum a single prominent 'Refresh everything now' button on the customer-facing connectors page), and set honest expectations in copy that the tool needs periodic refresh.

**16. ⛔ The only 'Refresh all sources' / 'Run today's reading' control lives on operator diagnostics — unreachable to me, so my data silently goes stale**
- *Surface:* operator diagnostics bleed — /diagnostics/connectors (the only refresh-all)
- *Why it's bad:* Ground-truth says the refresh-everything and per-platform 'run today's AI reading' controls live on /diagnostics/connectors, which is operator-gated (404 for customers) and not in my nav. With crons off and no autopilot, that means my connected sources just quietly stop updating, and the one button that would refresh them is hidden from me. I think I'm being tracked for 6 months; really my dashboard freezes the day after I connect.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no customer-reachable refresh-all; only operator-gated src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Surface a customer-facing 'Refresh now' control in the normal shell (connectors or Today), with clear last-refreshed timestamps so I can see when data is stale and fix it from my phone.

**17. ⛔ The only place to actually pull data and run AI readings 404s for me (operator-only)**
- *Surface:* /diagnostics/connectors (the only data-pull + AI-reading controls)
- *Why it's bad:* The architecture is fully on-demand — to get any data or recommendations someone must click "Refresh all connected sources" and "Run today's AI reading". Those buttons exist ONLY on /diagnostics/connectors, which calls notFound() unless BEACON_OPERATOR_MODE env is true. As a normal customer I will never have that env set, so the entire engine that makes Beacon work is behind a 404 wall. I connect everything and the product is physically incapable of producing a single result for me.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx lines 69-72 (notFound if !isOperatorModeServer) + src/lib/operator-mode.ts lines 48-50
- *Fix:* Expose customer-safe "Refresh data" and "Run today's reading" actions on a non-operator surface (e.g. the dashboard or /settings/connectors), or restore real autopilot. The data engine cannot live exclusively behind an operator-only 404.

**18. ⛔ "Beacon is preparing your first reading... tomorrow morning" is a lie — crons are off, no reading ever comes**
- *Surface:* first-reading-waiting screen (post-launch /)
- *Why it's bad:* Right after launch I land on "Beacon is preparing your first reading" with "Next reading: tomorrow morning". I take it at face value, close the tab, and come back tomorrow expecting a dashboard full of insights. Nothing has changed because crons are off and the only thing that triggers a reading is an operator-only 404 page. I've been promised an automatic event that the product structurally cannot deliver. This is the exact set-and-forget expectation I had, broken on day one.

- *Fix:* Either (a) restore a real scheduled reading so "tomorrow morning" is true, or (b) change the copy to reflect the on-demand reality and give me a button to trigger the first reading myself. Hardcoding "tomorrow morning" while crons are off is dishonest.

**19. ⛔ There is no path for me to reach ANY first value on my own within the first session**
- *Surface:* End-to-end time-to-first-value
- *Why it's bad:* Stitching it together from my lens: I sign up, do 4 builder-flavored steps, hit Launch, get a 404 (then maybe the dashboard), see "first reading tomorrow morning" that never comes, can't find the Connectors page, and even if I do, connecting GSC/Wix produces no data because the only refresh/AI-reading controls 404 for me. With LLM drafting off, even the recommendations that would appear are mostly empty. There is literally no sequence of clicks available to a normal first-time user that yields a single real insight or edit. The product cannot deliver first value to me at all.

- *Fix:* Define and wire one golden customer path that works end-to-end without operator env: signup → wizard → connect (linked) → customer-accessible "pull data & run reading" → real recommendations. Verify it by clicking through as a non-operator user. Today that path is broken at multiple independent points.

So the code to pull my GSC/GA4/Profound/Clarity/SEMrush data is fully built and just... not connected to any click. My keys sit there doing nothing.

But every data pull (Refresh all, per-source Sync now, 'Run today's AI reading' per platform, Recompute proof) is a manual button (diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx) and the codebase comments repeatedly state 'crons are off / no nightly job.' Connecting a source promises nothing recurring — copy like 'pulls fresh data on demand' on the SEMrush card is the only honest hint, and it's buried. **23. ⛔ The 'Run ChatGPT/Perplexity reading' buttons spend real paid-LLM money with no cost shown before OR after**
- *Surface:* /diagnostics/connectors — 'Run today's AI reading' buttons
- *Why it's bad:* These two buttons call runNativePoll, which hits paid OpenAI/Perplexity APIs ($0.012 and $0.005 per observation × every tracked prompt × platforms). The only cost disclosure is a buried comment 'Uses paid LLM APIs (operator-authorized)' in a code comment I'll never see. The buttons themselves say nothing about cost. Worse: the form actions are VOID-returning (runOpenAiReadingFromForm just calls the action and discards the result), so even the costEstimateUsd / observations count that runNativePoll computes is thrown away — after I click and pay, I see literally nothing. I'm flying blind on the exact action that burns the most money.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx (lines 159-176; cost note only in code comment lines 145-149); actions.ts runOpenAiReadingFromForm/runPerplexityReadingFromForm (lines 309-319) discard RunReadingResult
- *Fix:* Show an estimated cost next to each button (tracked-prompt count × per-obs rate), and convert the void form actions to useActionState so the returned observations + costEstimateUsd render after the run ('Ran ChatGPT — 47 prompts, ~$0.56'). Add a confirm dialog with the estimate.

**24. ⛔ I expect set-and-forget for 6 months, but every paid data pull requires me to manually click — and the manual surface is operator-gated**
- *Surface:* Autopilot expectation vs reality (crons off)

In reality crons are OFF: fresh data and new recs only happen when someone clicks 'Refresh all connected sources' and 'Run today's AI reading.' Those buttons live ONLY on /diagnostics/connectors, which is operator-mode-gated and 404s for a normal customer (notFound() at line 70-72). So as a real customer I literally cannot trigger the data pulls at all — my dashboard quietly goes stale forever while I think it's working. The cost angle: the only way to control spend (manual, per-click) is also the only way to get value, and I can't reach it.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx lines 69-72 (isOperatorModeServer → notFound); ground-truth: crons off, on-demand only
- *Fix:* Either give customers a real (cost-capped, cost-visible) scheduled autopilot, or expose a customer-facing 'Refresh data' + 'Run AI reading' surface with cost-before-click and a monthly cap. Don't gate the only data-pull mechanism behind operator mode.

**25. ⛔ There is no scheduling/auto-publish at all — I wanted set-and-forget for 6 months and instead must hand-click every single edit forever**
- *Surface:* Autopilot expectation vs reality
- *Why it's bad:* executePush is per-card and the architecture is crons-off / on-demand. The page even hard-states 'Each click publishes ONE card (Invariant 1)' (diagnostics/wix/page.tsx:199) and the batch helper is 'Accept all queued (≤20) — staging only, never publishes' (page.tsx:210). There is no schedule, no auto-approve rules, no 'publish low-risk edits automatically.' For me, 'mostly set-and-forget for 6 months' is impossible: I'd have to log into an operator-gated diagnostics page and individually click-publish edits, indefinitely.
