**86. ⚠️ 'Refresh all' computes a detailed per-connector refreshed/skipped/failed result that the UI throws on the floor**
- *Surface:* refreshAllDataSources partial-failure result
- *Why it's bad:* refreshAllDataSources returns a rich RefreshAllDataSourcesResult with results[], refreshedCount, skippedCount, failedCount per connector. But the page binds refreshAllDataSourcesFromForm, a void wrapper that awaits and discards the result. So when GSC refreshes fine but Profound FAILS (failedCount=1), I see nothing — the form just re-renders. The whole 'so the operator sees exactly what happened' design intent (their own comment) is discarded at the last step. Partial failures are invisible.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Convert the form to a client action (useTransition/useActionState) that renders the per-connector outcomes — especially failedCount with each failed provider + detail.

**87. ⚠️ If I leave GSC untouched >7 days, the next refresh silently returns 'no_usable_gsc_token' and is classified as a harmless skip, not a failure**
- *Surface:* GSC sync — token stale_over_7d path
- *Why it's bad:* This is my exact nightmare. evaluateExpiry returns stale_over_7d after 7 days and the sync won't even attempt a refresh — resolveGscAccessToken returns null → {synced:false, reason:'no_usable_gsc_token'}. In refreshAllDataSources, 'no_usable_gsc_token' is in NOT_CONNECTED_REASONS, so it's classified as 'skipped' (= 'not connected'), NOT 'failed.' But I AM connected — my token just aged out. The system tells me 'not connected, no big deal' when the truth is 'your core data source is dead and you must reconnect.' Set-and-forget for 6 months means GSC is dead within week 2 and nothing screams.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/actions.ts
- *Fix:* Distinguish 'never connected' from 'connected-but-expired/stale.' When a token exists but is stale_over_7d, classify as 'failed' (or a dedicated 'reconnect_required') and surface a loud 'Reconnect Google Search Console' prompt.

**88. ⚠️ Profound success with zero rows is indistinguishable from Profound failure — I can't tell 'no data' from 'broken'**
- *Surface:* Profound sync — 'returns nothing'
- *Why it's bad:* My persona obsesses over 'Profound returns nothing.' syncProfoundNightlyForTenant returns {synced:true, citation_rows:0, visibility_rows:0} when the API responds but every report is empty, AND it returns {synced:false, reason:'no_key_or_api_error'} when the key/API genuinely fails — and that reason is bucketed as a harmless 'skipped'. So whether Profound is misconfigured, the plan doesn't include the data, or it's truly empty, I get the same quiet non-signal. The summarizeConnectorSync helper even says 'Sync did not complete (not connected yet?)' guessing at the reason. I'm left guessing whether to debug my Profound account or wait.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/connectors/profound/sync-nightly.ts
- *Fix:* Split Profound's no_key_or_api_error into separate reasons (auth_failed vs api_error vs no_data) and surface 'Synced, 0 citations found' distinctly from 'Profound key/API failed — check your key.'

**89. ⚠️ Clarity needs manual refresh 'every couple of days' or history has permanent gaps — and nothing reminds me**
- *Surface:* Clarity connector
- *Why it's bad:* The Clarity card warns 'Clarity only shares the last 1-3 days, so refresh every couple of days to build history without gaps,' and sync-daily-metrics.ts confirms it stamps only YESTERDAY's date with no backfill. In a crons-off, set-and-forget world, I will absolutely forget to manually click refresh every 2 days. The result is permanent, unrecoverable holes in my friction history — and there's no reminder, no nudge, no auto-attempt. The product hands me a chore it knows I'll fail and then loses data forever when I do.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/connectors/clarity/sync-daily-metrics.ts
- *Fix:* Either re-enable a scheduled Clarity pull, or add an explicit staleness nudge ('Clarity data gap forming — refresh now to avoid losing this week') driven off the last clarity_daily_url_metrics date.

**90. ⚠️ Google can return no refresh_token and the callback still reports 'connected successfully' — guaranteeing a silent death later**
- *Surface:* OAuth callback — missing refresh_token
- *Why it's bad:* In the callback route, if tokens.refresh_token is absent it just log.warn's 'user may need to re-authorize with prompt=consent' and saves refresh_token:'' anyway, then redirects to ?connected=google_gsc → I see 'Google Search Console connected successfully.' But with no refresh token, my access token dies in ~1 hour and can never be refreshed — so my 'successful' connection is dead on arrival and I won't find out until data silently stops. For a 6-month set-and-forget user this is a landmine planted at connect time.
- *Grounding:* src/app/api/connectors/google/callback/route.ts
- *Fix:* If refresh_token is missing, do NOT report plain success — redirect with a warning state that tells me to reconnect with consent, or force prompt=consent on the auth URL so a refresh token is always returned.

**91. ⚠️ Clarity card tells me to 'refresh every couple of days' but gives me no refresh button**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors — Clarity card
- *Why it's bad:* The Clarity card copy (connectors-client.tsx lines 1201-1203) instructs: 'Clarity only shares the last 1-3 days, so refresh every couple of days to build history without gaps.' But the connected Clarity card only renders a 'Disconnect' button (line 1208) — there is no 'Sync now'/'Refresh' control anywhere on it. So the product gives me a maintenance chore ('refresh every couple of days') and then provides no way to do it. As a set-and-forget user this is maddening: it's both a manual burden AND impossible to satisfy, guaranteeing the data gaps the copy warns about.
- *Fix:* Add a working 'Sync now' button to the connected Clarity card wired to syncClarityNow. Until that exists, delete the 'refresh every couple of days' instruction so the UI doesn't ask me to do something it doesn't let me do.

**92. ⚠️ If I connect GSC but skip prompts, I fall through the first-reading guard into an empty/broken dashboard with no guidance**
- *Surface:* / (Today) — first-reading detection gap
- *Why it's bad:* detectFirstReadingState (first-reading-state.ts lines 93-95) returns isFirstReading:FALSE when activePromptCount <= 0. The friendly waiting screen ONLY shows when I have active prompts AND zero observations. But a GSC-led user (the exact pivot the codebase keeps describing) might launch with prompts toggled off or connect GSC as their primary signal. In that case I get neither the waiting screen nor real data — I fall through to the regular dashboard render, which for a fresh tenant means empty command-center cards and the bare 'Welcome to Beacon' card. The guiding empty state I need is conditioned on the wrong signal (prompts) rather than 'has this tenant produced any reading from any source yet'.
- *Fix:* Broaden the first-run waiting/guidance state to trigger on 'active tenant with no data from ANY connected source yet' (GSC, observations, etc.), not just 'has prompts but no observations'. Ensure there's always exactly one honest guiding screen for a zero-data active tenant.

**93. ⚠️ The palette is navigation-only — no command verbs, so I can't trigger Accept / Refresh / Run reading from ⌘K**
- *Surface:* Command palette (⌘K)
- *Why it's bad:* The whole point of a command palette for a power user is running commands without the mouse. command-palette.tsx PaletteItem only has an href and does router.push(item.href) (line 125-128). There is no 'action' item type. So I can't ⌘K → 'Refresh all sources', ⌘K → 'Run today's AI reading', ⌘K → 'Accept all recs'. In a crons-off, on-demand product where I must manually trigger data pulls, the absence of command verbs in the palette is a daily tax.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/command-palette.tsx
- *Fix:* Extend PaletteItem with an optional onRun action; add command items (Refresh sources, Run reading, Accept all, Toggle v2) so the palette executes, not just routes.

**94. ⚠️ 'Brain status' and 'Latest reading' operator cards bleed into my home screen**
- *Surface:* / Command Center (legacy Today)
- *Why it's bad:* The legacy Today Command Center is 5 cards including 'Brain status' and 'Latest reading' (command-center.tsx:5). 'Brain status' is meaningless to me as a business owner — it's system telemetry. Operator/diagnostic concepts surfacing on my main dashboard make me feel I'm looking at a machine's control panel instead of 'what should I do today,' which adds noise I have to filter past.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/components/today/command-center.tsx:5 (Five cards: Brain status, Latest reading, Top movement...)
- *Fix:* Strip 'Brain status' and raw telemetry cards from the customer Today; keep them on /diagnostics/brain only.

**95. ▫︎ The Data-sources cockpit prints 'last refreshed {raw timestamp}' with no human formatting and 'never refreshed' as the default**
- *Surface:* connectors cockpit — 'last refreshed' shows raw value
- *Why it's bad:* diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx line 122-124 renders `last refreshed ${r.lastSyncedAt}` — the raw stored string, not a formatted/relative date like the rest of the app uses. And because no customer sync ever runs, every row reads 'never refreshed' permanently. The one screen meant to prove 'the pull landed' instead shows a wall of 'never refreshed', confirming to me that my expensive connectors are doing nothing.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Format lastSyncedAt as a relative time (reuse formatDate from connectors-client) and ensure customer syncs actually update it so the row stops saying 'never refreshed.'

**96. ▫︎ The 'Data sources' cockpit lists 6 connectors but not Wix — so my publishing connection has no health/last-synced visibility here**
- *Surface:* connectors cockpit — Wix missing from the refresh batch
- *Why it's bad:* diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx CONNECTORS array (lines 44-50) includes GSC, GA4, Clarity, Profound, CallRail, Semrush — but NOT Wix. Wix is the connector that actually changes my live site, and it's invisible on the one-place-to-see-everything cockpit. I can't tell from here whether my publish pipe is connected or when it last did anything.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Add Wix to the cockpit rows (read-only status + last push from the push ledger) so the publishing connection is visible alongside the data connectors.

**97. ▫︎ Prompt detail promises the prompt runs 'on the next daily check' — same phantom-cron promise on a drilldown**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] detail
- *Why it's bad:* prompts/[id]/page.tsx line 806: tracks this 'prompt on the next daily check.' Every layer of the prompts feature reinforces the belief that there's a daily check that includes my prompts. There isn't. The lie is consistent and deep, which makes it more convincing and more damaging — I genuinely believe my prompt is queued for tonight.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Reframe to on-demand language consistently across all prompt surfaces ('included next time you run a reading').

**98. ▫︎ The always-visible freshness strip can render '(scheduled)' for a future timestamp, implying a scheduled run that doesn't exist**
- *Surface:* data-freshness-strip (shell-wide)
- *Why it's bad:* data-freshness-strip.tsx line 65-66: when ageDays < 0 it labels the time '(scheduled)'. This strip sits at the top of the shell on every page. Any future-dated import/scan timestamp makes the whole app header claim something is 'scheduled' — reinforcing, app-wide, the false belief that Beacon runs on a schedule. Constant low-grade misinformation in my peripheral vision.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/components/shell/data-freshness-strip.tsx
- *Fix:* Remove the 'scheduled' label (or only show it when a real schedule is configured). In a manual-only build there is nothing scheduled.

**99. ▫︎ More 'Check back tomorrow' / 'after the next daily check' empty states reinforcing the dead cron**
- *Surface:* /today-visibility-snapshot + today-scoreboard
- *Why it's bad:* today-visibility-snapshot.tsx line 190 ('Check back tomorrow.') and today-scoreboard.tsx line 136 ('Beacon starts collecting AI answers after the next daily check.'). Every empty state on the dashboard points me at a daily check that won't run. Collectively these make the empty/early experience feel like 'just wait, it's working' when nothing is working. I wait, churn, and never learn I had to click a hidden button.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/components/today/today-visibility-snapshot.tsx
- *Fix:* Sweep ALL Today empty states to on-demand language with a visible refresh CTA, not 'check back tomorrow.'

**100. ▫︎ The recommendations executive strip also references 'the next daily reading' as if recs auto-refresh**
- *Surface:* executive-strip (recommendations header)
- *Why it's bad:* recommendations/executive-strip.tsx line 119 references 'the next daily reading.' The header of my action queue tells me the queue is tied to an automatic daily refresh. So I treat the recs list as a living, self-updating feed I can ignore between logins — when in fact it's a frozen snapshot until I manually trigger generation (which I can't, from the customer UI). The strip oversells freshness on the exact surface where I make decisions.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/components/recommendations/executive-strip.tsx
- *Fix:* Reframe the strip to reflect when the queue was actually generated and how to refresh it on demand.

**101. ▫︎ Definition lists use a fixed 140px label column (grid-cols-[140px_1fr]) — squeezes values into a sliver on a 360px phone**
- *Surface:* first-reading 'what Beacon learned' + waiting panels — src/components/today/first-reading-waiting.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* Both dl grids (lines 51 and 81) are `grid-cols-[140px_1fr]`. On a 360px screen minus 24px padding ×2, a fixed 140px label column leaves ~170px for the value — so my business name, areas served, and domain wrap awkwardly or truncate. Fixed-pixel label columns are a desktop habit that breaks on narrow screens.
- *Grounding:* src/components/today/first-reading-waiting.tsx
- *Fix:* Use a responsive label column (e.g. stack to a single column under sm, or use grid-cols-[minmax(90px,auto)_1fr]) so values get room on a phone.

**102. ▫︎ The brief is structured as a 5-act long-scroll with 10px uppercase labels — heavy reading for a between-customers glance**
- *Surface:* /recommendations detail evidence grid — src/app/(shell)/recommendations/[id]/recommendation-detail-client.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* The detail page is an 'Act 1...Act 5' brief with sections, 10px uppercase tracking labels (e.g. line 366), and a sm:grid-cols-2 evidence grid that stacks to a tall single column on mobile. Deciding whether to approve a live website change means scrolling through acts of dense small-caps copy on my phone. I want the gist and an approve button; instead I get a multi-screen essay.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/recommendations/[id]/recommendation-detail-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a mobile-first summary header at the top (the change + a big Approve button) so I can decide without scrolling the full 5-act brief, and bump the 10px section labels to a legible size.

**103. ▫︎ Competitors step asks me to name rivals manually, then says it does it automatically anyway**
- *Surface:* /onboard/competitors
- *Why it's bad:* The whole step is "Pick the competitors you want Beacon to compare you against" — but the field label then admits "(optional — Beacon finds your real AI-answer rivals automatically once tracking starts)". So I burn a full wizard step racking my brain for competitor names, only to be told it's optional and the system does it better automatically. It feels like make-work that pads the funnel and makes me doubt whether any of my inputs matter.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/competitors/competitors-form.tsx lines 63-72 (label contradicts the step's purpose)
- *Fix:* Either demote competitors to an optional inline field on an existing step, or lead with the auto-discovery value and frame manual entry as "add any you already know". Don't make a dedicated step for something you advertise as automatic and optional.

**104. ▫︎ The "What Beacon learned" block silently vanishes when the site scan fails**
- *Surface:* first-reading-waiting → "What Beacon learned from your site"
- *Why it's bad:* The one bit of instant gratification — Beacon showing it read my site (industry, areas, services) — only renders if derivation produced something. The launch flow is failure-soft: an unreachable site or a throw just launches without it, with zero message to me. So after onboarding I may see nothing about my site and have no idea whether Beacon even looked at it or quietly failed. The magic moment is conditional and unexplained.
- *Grounding:* src/components/today/first-reading-waiting.tsx lines 72-117 (block only renders when derived has content) + src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts lines 191-204 (failure-soft, silent)
- *Fix:* Always render the block with an honest state: either what was learned, or "We couldn't read your site automatically — check your URL or add details in Settings." Don't silently hide a failed scan.

**105. ▫︎ The operator data-sources page prints raw ISO timestamps as 'last refreshed' and GSC always reads 'never refreshed'**
- *Surface:* /diagnostics/connectors freshness column
- *Why it's bad:* loadRows renders `last refreshed ${r.lastSyncedAt}` (page.tsx line 123) — a raw ISO string, not a human date — and because GSC sync never writes last_synced_at, GSC perpetually shows 'never refreshed' here even after I refresh-all and it returns rows. The one page that's supposed to prove 'the pull landed' actively undermines its own promise.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/connectors/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Format lastSyncedAt as a relative/local time and fix the GSC writer so this row reflects real pulls.

**106. ▫︎ Clarity quietly requires me to manually refresh every 1-3 days forever or my history gets permanent gaps**
- *Surface:* Clarity card
- *Why it's bad:* The Clarity card copy (line 1202) admits 'Clarity only shares the last 1-3 days, so refresh every couple of days to build history without gaps.' But there's no auto-refresh and (per the other findings) the customer-facing refresh button isn't even wired. So a set-and-forget user WILL get permanent holes in Clarity friction history, and the only mitigation is a chore the UI doesn't currently let me do.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Auto-schedule the daily Clarity pull (it's budgeted to 1/day) rather than asking me to remember a manual refresh every couple days; until then, wire a working Clarity 'Sync now'.

**107. ▫︎ Disconnecting says 'cached data preserved' but reconnecting/refreshing to get fresh data re-spends, and that's never flagged**
- *Surface:* Connectors page — disconnect preserves cached data, but no cost of re-pull shown
- *Why it's bad:* Every disconnect tooltip reassures me 'historical data stays cached but no new data refreshes until you reconnect.' Sounds thrifty. But the unspoken half is: to get current data again I must reconnect AND run a paid refresh/reading, which costs again. The UI frames disconnect as free and safe, but never tells me that the round-trip back to fresh data has a dollar cost. As a cost-watcher I'd happily disconnect to 'save money' and then get surprised by the re-pull bill.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx lines 619, 775 (disconnect tooltips); reconnect/refresh cost never disclosed
- *Fix:* Add to the reconnect/refresh path a note that pulling fresh data after a disconnect re-spends, with the estimate, so the cost of toggling is explicit.

**108. ▫︎ No way to schedule a push for a safe time or stage it — every publish is immediate, live, now**
- *Surface:* Approve & Push (no scheduling/quiet-hours)
- *Why it's bad:* executePush writes immediately on click; there is no 'publish tonight', 'stage to a Wix draft/sandbox', or 'hold for my review window' option anywhere in the push path. For blog content there's a draft path (wixCreateDraftPost) but for the CMS field edits I care about it's instant-live only. I can't push during low-traffic hours, can't stage to a non-public draft to eyeball it first — it's all-or-nothing the moment I click.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/push/push-service.ts
- *Fix:* Offer a 'stage as Wix draft / schedule for later' option for supported surfaces so I can review the real thing in Wix before it's public, instead of every push hitting the live page instantly.

**109. ▫︎ Live-changes blocks say Beacon 'is collecting post-change readings' — implying background work that's off**
- *Surface:* /today (live-changes copy)
- *Why it's bad:* src/domains/today/live-changes-data.ts pickStateCopy returns lines like 'Live change detected — too recent for a confidence picture' (RECENT_COPY) and PRE_VERDICT_COPY about Beacon collecting readings. daysSinceLive can be 13 after my absence, yet the copy frames it as in-flight background collection. Nothing is collecting — readings only happen when I manually run them. So my shipped changes look like they're being actively measured when they're actually frozen in time.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/today/live-changes-data.ts
- *Fix:* Make pre-verdict / recent copy crons-off aware: surface 'no readings recorded since this shipped — run a reading to get a verdict' instead of implying ongoing collection.

**110. ▫︎ Classification only looks back 7 days but the page fetches 14 days — and 'primary' needs 3+ readings I have no way to generate on demand**
- *Surface:* /prompts (descriptor/answer-shape lookback)
- *Why it's bad:* opportunity-classify.ts defaults lookbackDays:7 and minObservationsForCategory:3 (lines 96-99). The page fetches 14 days (page.tsx line 88) 'with margin' but the classifier only uses 7. With crons off and the poll button unreachable to me (see other finding), I can't accumulate 3 readings in 7 days, so most prompts are stuck 'Early' / 'Still learning' permanently. The thresholds assume a daily poll loop that doesn't run for me.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/prompts/opportunity-classify.ts
- *Fix:* Lower the min-observations bar OR make the lookback window adapt to actual poll cadence, so an on-demand user isn't permanently stranded in 'Early'.

**111. ▫︎ After I connect Profound there's no Sync/Refresh button on the card and no 'last synced' freshness**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors (Profound card)
- *Why it's bad:* connectors-client.tsx Profound card (lines 1123-1181) only renders Connect (when disconnected) and Disconnect (when connected) plus 'Authorized DATE'. There is no 'Sync now' and no 'data last refreshed X ago'. The product map confirms syncProfoundNow exists in actions but is wired to no button. So I connect my AI-visibility source and then have zero way to pull or even see how stale my AI data is from this card. For my core connector that's unacceptable.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a 'Sync now' button + a 'last synced' timestamp to the Profound card (and wire the existing syncProfoundNow action).

**112. ▫︎ Clarity card admits it only has 1-3 days of data and tells me to manually refresh 'every couple of days' — that's not set-and-forget**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors (Clarity freshness expectation)
- *Why it's bad:* connectors-client.tsx lines 1199-1203: 'Clarity only shares the last 1-3 days, so refresh every couple of days to build history without gaps.' I connected everything to set-and-forget for 6 months. This card openly tells me I must come back and manually refresh every 2 days or my page-friction history gets holes. There's no auto-refresh and (per the product map) the Clarity sync button isn't even wired. So the honest copy just documents that the product can't keep this data current on its own.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Add scheduled Clarity polling (or at minimum a working one-click refresh) so I don't have to babysit it every 48 hours to avoid gaps.

**113. ▫︎ If I have >10 Profound categories, only the first 10 ever sync — and I only find out by reading server logs I can't see**
- *Surface:* Profound category cap
- *Why it's bad:* sync-nightly.ts caps at MAX_CATEGORIES_PER_NIGHT=10 and the overflow is only a log.warn 'category cap hit — syncing first batch only.' That log is server-side; I never see it. The returned result is {synced:true, categories:10} with no 'X categories skipped' field. So with 15 categories, 5 are permanently never pulled and the UI shows happy success. My AI-visibility data is quietly incomplete with no customer-visible signal.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/connectors/profound/sync-nightly.ts
- *Fix:* Add a skipped-categories count to ProfoundSyncResult and surface 'Synced 10 of 15 categories — N not pulled' so the cap is honest at the UI, not just in logs.

**114. ▫︎ Critical 'refresh every couple days or you lose history' instruction is buried in tiny muted text**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors — Clarity card
- *Why it's bad:* The Clarity card warns 'Clarity only shares the last 1-3 days, so refresh every couple of days to build history without gaps' — a make-or-break operational instruction for a 'set and forget' user — in 12px muted-foreground. For me this reads as a faint grey blur I'd skip entirely, then wonder for months why my Clarity history is full of holes.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx (Clarity copy line 1198-1205, text-[12px] text-muted-foreground)
- *Fix:* Promote this instruction to readable body size and normal foreground contrast, and surface it near the connect action, not only in the footer fine print.


## Onboarding & first value (33)

**115. ⛔ After signup I have NO link anywhere to connect Wix, Google, or any tool**
- *Surface:* /today, /recommendations, /settings (navigation)
- *Why it's bad:* navigation.ts has only Today/Recommendations/Prompts/Changes/Settings (no Connectors). settings-tabs-client.tsx (lines 12-17) shows only Import/Config/Prompts/Data — Connectors is explicitly removed. /settings redirects to /settings/prompts. The whole pitch on the signup page is 'Connect the tools you already use' but the connect page (/settings/connectors) is orphaned: I'd have to magically type the URL. I connected SEMrush/Profound/Wix/GSC/GA4/Clarity expecting a guided flow and there isn't even a visible door to it.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/navigation.ts
- *Fix:* Add 'Connectors' back to the Settings tab bar and/or the main nav, and link to /settings/connectors from the first-reading-waiting screen. The single most important page for a new customer is currently unreachable by clicking.

**116. ⛔ As a bakery owner my whole launch produces only 2 prompts ('Bakery reviews', 'is Bakery a good company')**
- *Surface:* /onboard/review (Step 4) + first-value
- *Why it's bad:* The review page (onboard/review/page.tsx lines 66-72) calls generateStarterPrompts WITHOUT passing derivedServices or industry. The builder service-in-city prompts (generator lines 184-242) only fire for the six ProjectMixTag builder tags — which I correctly leave blank because I'm a bakery. The site-derived path that would give me 'best bakery in [city]' (lines 244-298) needs derivedServices/industry, which the review page never passes. Net: I launch with 2 generic brand prompts and nothing about my actual product. The product looks empty and useless on day one — the exact moment I decide whether to keep paying.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/onboarding/prompt-generator.ts
- *Fix:* Pass derivedServices + industry (from the homepage crawl / BusinessConfig) into generateStarterPrompts on the review page so a bakery sees 'best bakery in <city>' etc. in the launch preview, not just brand prompts.

**117. ⛔ Launching Beacon redirects me to /today, which 404s**
- *Surface:* /onboard/review → /today redirect
- *Why it's bad:* launch-form.tsx and launch-flow.ts both redirect to '/today' after I click 'Launch Beacon', but ground truth is the dashboard lives at '/' and '/today' 404s. So the very first thing I see after finishing onboarding is a Not Found page. There is no worse first impression for a brand-new paying customer than the launch button dumping them on a 404.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts
- *Fix:* Change the post-launch redirect (and the signup/auth comments referencing /today) to '/'. Audit every redirect('/today') in the codebase.

**118. ⛔ Clicking "Launch Beacon" redirects to /today, which is a 404**
- *Surface:* /onboard/review → Launch Beacon button
- *Why it's bad:* This is THE moment of first value. I fill out 4 steps, tick the consent box, hit the big black "Launch Beacon" button expecting my dashboard — and I get a Next.js 404 page. There is no /today directory in the app; the dashboard lives at "/". My very first impression of the product after committing my email, business, cities and competitors is a broken page. I bounce immediately and assume the whole thing is dead.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts (lines 142, 327, 349 redirect to:"/today") + actions.ts line 88 redirect(outcome.to)
- *Fix:* In src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts and actions.ts, change every redirect target from "/today" to "/" (the real dashboard route). Same for auth/callback. Add a route-existence test so the launch redirect can never point at a non-existent path again.

**119. ⛔ Signup promises "Connect the tools you already use" but the wizard never lets me connect anything**
- *Surface:* Onboarding wizard (all 4 steps) vs /signup promise
- *Why it's bad:* The signup card literally sells me on "Connect the tools you already use; Beacon finds evidence-backed edits...". I connected SEMrush, Profound, Wix, GSC, GA4, Clarity in my head before I even started. But the 4-step wizard is Business → Service area → Competitors → Review. There is no connect step, no mention of GSC/GA4/Wix/SEMrush/Profound/Clarity anywhere in onboarding. I finish onboarding having connected zero of the six tools the product is built around, and nobody told me where to do it.
- *Grounding:* src/components/onboard/onboarding-shell.tsx (STEP_LABELS only: Business/Service area/Competitors/Review) + src/app/(public)/signup/page.tsx lines 38-44
- *Fix:* Add a 5th onboarding step (or a prominent post-launch checklist) that walks me through connecting GSC/GA4/Wix/SEMrush/Profound/Clarity, linking to /settings/connectors. At minimum, the Review step and FirstReadingWaiting screen must surface a "Connect your data sources" CTA.

**120. ⛔ There is no customer email digest at all — I get NOTHING in my inbox after 2 weeks away**
- *Surface:* email digest / /settings (notifications)
- *Why it's bad:* I was promised 'set-and-forget for 6 months.' The whole point of leaving is that Beacon reaches OUT to me. But src/domains/onboarding/provision-tenant.ts line 59 sets every new tenant's email_frequency to 'off' by default, and there is literally no settings UI anywhere to turn it on (grep of src/app + src/components finds email_frequency only in the static methodology page). The one digest that exists (src/domains/delivery/morning-digest.ts) is explicitly 'the OPERATOR digest' that goes to a single BEACON_DIGEST_TO inbox — its own doc comment says the per-tenant email_frequency field 'is deliberately NOT consulted here.' So as a paying customer I will never, ever receive an email. I came back to a cold app with zero idea anything happened.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/onboarding/provision-tenant.ts
- *Fix:* Default email_frequency to 'weekly' for new tenants, add a Notifications section in Settings to control it, and wire a per-tenant customer digest (not just the operator one) that sends to the tenant's own member email via resend.ts.

**121. ⛔ Clicking 'Launch Beacon' redirects me to /today, which does not exist — my very first action 404s**
- *Surface:* /onboard/review → Launch Beacon (redirect target)
- *Why it's bad:* I just finished a 4-step wizard, ticked the consent box, and hit the one big button. launch-flow.ts returns `to: "/today"` in all three outcome branches (lines 142, 327, 349) and the form comment says 'success → server action calls redirect("/today")'. But there is NO /today route — the dashboard lives at "/", and there is no rewrite/redirect for /today in next.config or middleware. So the literal first thing Beacon does after I commit is throw me at a 404. For a brand-new user with zero data, that is the worst possible first impression: it screams 'this product is broken' before I've seen a single screen.
- *Fix:* Change the three `to: "/today"` values in src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts to `to: "/"` (the actual dashboard route per src/lib/navigation.ts). Add a redirect/rewrite from /today → / in next.config as a safety net, and add a smoke test asserting the post-launch target resolves.

**122. ⚠️ Onboarding asks 'What kind of work do you take on?' with only builder options — none fit a bakery**
- *Surface:* /onboard/scope (Step 2 of onboarding)
- *Why it's bad:* PROJECT_MIX_TAGS (lines 30-46) are 'New construction', 'Whole-home remodel', 'Kitchen & bath', 'ADUs / additions', 'Teardown / rebuild', 'Mixed-use commercial / residential'. I run a bakery. Every single checkbox is for a home builder/contractor. On my SECOND onboarding screen the product is already telling me it wasn't built for me. It makes me distrust every recommendation that follows.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/onboarding/scope-validation.ts
- *Fix:* Remove the hardcoded builder tag grid (or make it industry-aware), and lean on the site-derived services instead. A bakery should never see 'Teardown / rebuild' as an option.

**123. ⚠️ The very first sentence I read is jargon: 'win AI citations and Google clicks' + 'evidence-backed edits' + 'paste-ready steps'**
- *Surface:* Signup page
- *Why it's bad:* The signup sub-copy (lines 38-44) says Beacon 'finds evidence-backed edits to win AI citations and Google clicks ... pushes the eligible edits live ... the rest come as paste-ready steps.' I don't know what an 'AI citation' is, and 'paste-ready steps' tells me I'll have to do manual developer work — the opposite of the set-and-forget I want. The first impression promises both magic ('pushes live') and homework ('paste-ready steps') in one breath, which is confusing and a little alarming.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(public)/signup/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Lead with the customer outcome in plain English: 'Beacon finds the changes that get more people to find your business on Google and AI, and makes them for you.' Save the mechanics for later, and don't front-load 'paste-ready steps.'

**124. ⚠️ The only service options are home-builder verticals — useless for my Wix business**
- *Surface:* /onboard/scope → "What kind of work do you take on?"
- *Why it's bad:* Step 2 asks what work I take on, and the only checkboxes are New construction, Whole-home remodel, Kitchen & bath, ADUs / additions, Teardown / rebuild, Mixed-use commercial/residential. I run a Wix site that is almost certainly not a custom-home builder. None of these describe me, so I leave them all blank and immediately feel like this product wasn't built for me — it's a builder tool wearing a generic costume. First-impression trust gone.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/onboarding/scope-validation.ts lines 30-46 (PROJECT_MIX_TAGS / PROJECT_MIX_LABELS are all builder verticals)
- *Fix:* Replace the hardcoded builder PROJECT_MIX_TAGS with vertical-agnostic categories, or drop the checkbox grid entirely in favor of services derived from my site. As-is it's leaked vertical assumption that tells non-builders to leave.

**125. ⚠️ If I skip the builder checkboxes and cities, the prompt preview is nearly empty and Launch warns me**
- *Surface:* /onboard/review → starter prompts preview
- *Why it's bad:* Because the service options don't fit me, I skip them. The starter-prompt generator's service-in-city, cost, and comparison families almost all key off the six builder tags + cities. With no builder tag selected I get maybe two brand prompts ("X reviews", "is X a good company") and possibly a "No starter prompts yet" / "We need at least one prompt to launch" warning. After 4 steps the product basically tells me it has nothing to track for my business. That's a brutal payoff for the effort.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/onboarding/prompt-generator.ts lines 184-242 (service-in-city/cost families gated on the six builder tags) + src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-form.tsx lines 77-82
- *Fix:* Lean on site-derived services/industry as the primary prompt source for non-builders (the derivedServices path exists but only fires at launch from the homepage crawl, not in the live preview). Show the derived prompts in the Review preview so non-builders see a full, relevant list before launching.

**126. ⚠️ I'm forced to agree to "Beacon's terms" twice, but there is no terms link or page anywhere**
- *Surface:* /signup + /onboard/review (TOS)
- *Why it's bad:* Signup says "By continuing you agree to Beacon's terms." The launch step makes me tick "I agree...". But "terms" is plain unlinked text and there is no /terms or /privacy route in the app at all. I'm being asked to legally agree to something I cannot read. For a first-time user handing over my email and authorizing edits to my live Wix site, that's an instant credibility hit — feels sketchy, like a weekend project.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(public)/signup/signup-form.tsx lines 60-63 (unlinked "terms") + no terms/privacy route exists (ABSENCE: src/app/terms, src/app/privacy)
- *Fix:* Add a real /terms (and /privacy) page and hyperlink "Beacon's terms" in both the signup form and the launch consent. Don't request agreement to invisible terms.

**127. ⚠️ "Save and sign out" doesn't sign me out — it bounces me right back into the app**
- *Surface:* Onboarding shell → "Save and sign out" link
- *Why it's bad:* Mid-onboarding I want to step away, so I click "Save and sign out". It's just a <Link href="/login">. Since I'm still authenticated, /login immediately redirects me to "/" — I'm NOT signed out, and I'm dumped somewhere I didn't ask to go. A control that flatly does the opposite of its label destroys my trust that any button in this product does what it says.
- *Grounding:* src/components/onboard/onboarding-shell.tsx lines 49-54 (Link to /login) + src/app/login/page.tsx lines 15-17 (authed user redirected to /)
- *Fix:* Point "Save and sign out" at the real sign-out handler (/auth/signout) instead of /login, so it actually signs me out.

**128. ⚠️ "We'll fetch your homepage" — but the fetch only happens silently at Launch with no result shown**
- *Surface:* /onboard/business → Website field
- *Why it's bad:* Step 1 hints "We'll fetch your homepage to learn how AI search engines describe you." That's the promise that hooks me. But the actual fetch (deriveAndPersistTenantConfig) runs invisibly inside the Launch transaction, ≤3 pages, and I only maybe see a tiny "What Beacon learned" block on the waiting screen IF derivation succeeded. There's no "Scanning your site..." moment, no preview between steps. The single most magical part of onboarding is hidden, so the wizard feels like a dumb form instead of something intelligent reading my site.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts lines 163-204 (config derivation buried in launch) + src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/business-form.tsx lines 121-124 (promise made early)
- *Fix:* Trigger the site scan right after the business step and show me what Beacon learned (industry, services, locations) before I continue — make the homepage fetch a visible, value-delivering moment, not a silent launch-time side effect.

**129. ⚠️ After "Check your email" there's no resend, no spam hint, and no way back**
- *Surface:* Magic-link signup flow
- *Why it's bad:* I enter my email, the form swaps to "Check your email. Click the link to finish creating your account." If the email is slow, lands in spam, or I typo'd my address, I'm stuck: no "resend link", no "wrong email? go back", no "didn't get it?" help. For a magic-link-only product (no password fallback), a missing email is a total dead-end on step zero. I just close the tab.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(public)/signup/signup-form.tsx lines 28-34 (sent state is a static message, no resend/back)
- *Fix:* On the sent state, add a "Resend link", an "Use a different email" reset, and a spam-folder hint. Magic-link with no recovery path is fragile for first-timers.

**130. ⚠️ 'No payment required to get started' implies free, but hides that I'll be spending real LLM/API money**
- *Surface:* /signup — pitch copy
- *Why it's bad:* The signup form's only money-related line is 'No payment required to get started.' That reads as 'this is free.' It is NOT free in any way I care about: the moment I run an AI reading or refresh SEMrush/Profound, I'm spending paid-API dollars (mine via my keys, and Beacon's OpenAI/Perplexity spend). 'No payment required to get started' is the kind of phrasing that lands me with a surprise bill — the exact thing I'm paranoid about. It sets a false expectation at the very first screen.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(public)/signup/signup-form.tsx lines 60-63
- *Fix:* Replace with honest framing: 'Free to set up. Running AI readings and data refreshes uses paid APIs — you'll always see the cost before you spend.' Then actually show the cost (see other findings).

**131. ⚠️ Onboarding makes me name my top rivals, then there's no screen that pits me directly against the one I named**
- *Surface:* /competitors / onboarding step 3
- *Why it's bad:* Onboarding Step 3 ('Competitors to watch') has me pick 1-5 rivals to 'compare you against'. I expect a clean head-to-head: me vs CompetitorOne across topics/prompts/citations. What actually exists is scattered — a leaderboard row, a collapsed battlecard, an empty /competitors/[id] profile (broken, see above). There is no single 'you vs [the rival I explicitly named]' dashboard. The product asked me who my enemy is and then never showed me the duel.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no head-to-head 'me vs one named competitor' comparison view despite onboarding asking me to pick rivals
- *Fix:* Build a head-to-head view keyed to the competitors I named in onboarding: side-by-side citation share, topics where each leads, prompts where they beat me, and their recent winning moves — one rival, one screen, with copyable counter-moves.

**132. ⚠️ Onboarding never mentions importing data, yet every empty state funnels me to CSV import — the two flows don't connect**
- *Surface:* /settings/import — demo banner destination after self-serve onboarding
- *Why it's bad:* The 4-step wizard (business → scope → competitors → review) is entirely about connecting tools and tracking prompts; it never asks for or mentions a data file. Yet immediately after launch, the demo/empty states on /, /competitors, /briefs/proposed all push me to /settings/import for a CSV/.xlsx I was never told to prepare. So the product's onboarding (connect tools) and its empty-state remediation (import a spreadsheet) are two disconnected mental models bolted together. As a new user I'm whipsawed: 'I just set up prompts and connectors — why is everything telling me to import a file?'
- *Fix:* Unify the new-user path. After launch, the empty states should reflect what onboarding actually set up (prompts + connectors) and drive me to trigger a reading / connect remaining tools — with CSV import as an explicit secondary 'or import historical data' option, not the only door.

**133. ⚠️ Launch redirects to /today, which 404s — I land on a broken URL right after paying attention**
- *Surface:* Onboarding review → Today
- *Why it's bad:* The launch transaction redirects to /today (per the documented flow), but ground truth is there is NO /today route — the dashboard is at '/'. After I carefully complete a 4-step wizard and hit 'Launch Beacon,' getting bounced to a 404 is the worst possible first impression and tells me the product can't even route its own happy path.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/launch-flow.ts (redirect target '/today') vs src/lib/navigation.ts:31 (Today href = '/')
- *Fix:* Redirect launch to '/' (the real dashboard). Add a /today → / redirect so no internal link can 404.

**134. ▫︎ Onboarding's only escape is 'Save and sign out' — there's no way to skip ahead or finish fast**
- *Surface:* Onboarding wizard (all 4 steps)
- *Why it's bad:* OnboardingShell (lines 49-54) offers exactly one footer action: 'Save and sign out' linking to /login. Every step (business, scope, competitors) forces me through forms full of builder/competitor questions before I can see ANY value. As a busy bakery owner I can't say 'just use my website, show me something.' The competitors step even asks me to name rivals I may not know off the top of my head. The wizard feels like a tax form, not a 2-minute setup.
- *Grounding:* src/components/onboard/onboarding-shell.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a 'Skip — just use my website' fast path that crawls my homepage and launches with site-derived prompts, so I can reach value in under a minute and fill in details later.

**135. ▫︎ Step 3 demands I type competitor company names from memory, with a confusing 'names not URLs' rule**
- *Surface:* /onboard/competitors (Step 3)
- *Why it's bad:* The field (lines 67-106) says 'Competitors to compare you against' and the hint says 'Names work best, not website URLs' and the validator rejects URL-shaped tokens. I think of my competitors by their website or their storefront, and now I'm being told my instinct (paste their site) is wrong. The parenthetical even admits 'Beacon finds your real AI-answer rivals automatically once tracking starts' — so why am I being made to guess at all? It's busywork that makes me feel I'm doing the tool's job.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/competitors/competitors-form.tsx
- *Fix:* Make this step truly optional and frictionless (or auto-suggest competitors from the site crawl), and accept pasted URLs by stripping them to a name instead of erroring.

**136. ▫︎ The recap can show "New Beacon Account" as my business name**
- *Surface:* /onboard/scope + /onboard/competitors → "Saved so far" recap
- *Why it's bad:* The scope and competitor steps render a "Saved so far" box echoing my business name from the tenant row. The provisioner seeds a placeholder "New Beacon Account". The business step blanks the placeholder in its input, but the downstream recap reads tenant.business_name directly — so if anything about the name save hiccups, I see "New Beacon Account" presented back to me as MY business. Looking at a generic placeholder where my company name should be makes the whole thing feel like a hollow template.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/scope/page.tsx line 43 (renders ctx.tenant.business_name raw) + src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/page.tsx lines 56-60 (placeholder guard not reused)
- *Fix:* Apply the same isPlaceholderBusinessName guard used in business/page.tsx to the recap blocks in scope/page.tsx and competitors/page.tsx (and the Review summary), rendering a dash or prompt instead of "New Beacon Account".

**137. ▫︎ No time estimate, no "what happens after" — I don't know what I'm signing up for**
- *Surface:* Onboarding wizard (all steps)
- *Why it's bad:* I judge products in 5 minutes. The wizard never tells me how long onboarding takes, how many fields per step, or what I'll get at the end. Each step just appears. With a 5-minute patience budget I want "Takes 2 minutes, then Beacon scans your site" up front. Without it, every step feels like it could be the start of an endless form, and I'm primed to bail.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no time/outcome estimate in src/components/onboard/onboarding-shell.tsx or src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a one-line expectation-setter on step 1 ("~2 minutes. We'll scan your site and start tracking how AI describes you.") and keep the step labels visible so I can see the finish line.

**138. ▫︎ Website field accepts free text with no normalization feedback — I can't tell if my Wix URL is right**
- *Surface:* /onboard/business → Website validation
- *Why it's bad:* The field placeholder is "acme.com" and accepts any string. My Wix site might be mysite.wixsite.com/foo or a connected custom domain. There's no inline confirmation that Beacon parsed my domain correctly, no "we'll scan https://..." echo. If I fat-finger it or paste the full Wix editor URL, I have no idea, and the whole downstream scan silently targets the wrong site. As a non-technical first-timer I need reassurance the URL landed right.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/business-form.tsx lines 96-126 (no normalized-domain echo)
- *Fix:* Echo the normalized domain Beacon will actually fetch (e.g. "We'll scan https://mysite.com") inline after I type, and flag obviously-wrong inputs (wix editor URLs, full paths) before I continue.

**139. ▫︎ Signup and login describe the product as two different things**
- *Surface:* /signup vs /login pitch copy
- *Why it's bad:* Signup says Beacon "finds evidence-backed edits to win AI citations and Google clicks." Login says Beacon "finds your site's SEO & AEO fixes." As someone forming a first impression, two pages with two different pitches (AI citations vs SEO/AEO jargon) makes me unsure what this product actually is. And "AEO" / "citations" is jargon I shouldn't have to decode in the first 30 seconds.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(public)/signup/page.tsx lines 38-44 vs src/app/login/page.tsx lines 24-29
- *Fix:* Unify the one-line pitch across /signup and /login in plain language ("Beacon shows you the exact website changes that get you recommended by AI and found on Google — and pushes the approved ones live"). Drop AEO/citations jargon from the entry copy.

**140. ▫︎ I'm told my progress is saved but nothing confirms it across steps**
- *Surface:* Onboarding → no progress persistence cue
- *Why it's bad:* The shell footer says "Need to step out? Save and sign out", implying my progress persists. But each step's submit just redirects forward; there's no "Saved" confirmation, no indication the previous step's data is safely stored. Combined with the recap sometimes showing placeholder data, I don't trust that closing the tab won't lose my work. For a cautious first-timer that uncertainty is enough to make me power through anxiously or abandon.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/business-form.tsx lines 36-44 (silent redirect on save, no saved confirmation)
- *Fix:* Show a brief "Saved" confirmation on each step transition and make the "Saved so far" recap reliable, so I trust I can leave and return without losing anything.

**141. ▫︎ Geo-first "Cities you serve" framing assumes I'm a local service business**
- *Surface:* /onboard/scope → cities field for a non-local Wix business
- *Why it's bad:* Step 2 leads with "Service area and services" and a big "Cities you serve" textarea. Many Wix sites (online stores, content sites, SaaS, portfolios) have no service area at all. The "(skip if location doesn't apply)" aside is buried in the label. The whole step is framed around local geography, reinforcing the builder/local-service assumption and making non-local businesses feel like they're filling out the wrong form.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/scope/page.tsx lines 32-36 + src/app/(shell)/onboard/scope/scope-form.tsx lines 88-93
- *Fix:* Make the step neutral: ask "Where are your customers?" with an explicit "I sell online / no specific area" option, and don't make cities the visual centerpiece for businesses that derive nothing from geo.

**142. ▫︎ The launch preview shows mono-spaced query strings with no explanation of why they matter**
- *Surface:* /onboard/review → starter prompts as raw search strings
- *Why it's bad:* The final review lists prompts like "best custom home builder in Atherton" in monospace font under jargon-y group headers, with one line "Drafted from your inputs — you can edit later." There's no per-prompt rationale shown (the generator computes one but the page doesn't render it). As a non-technical owner I'm staring at a list of search phrases not understanding what Beacon will DO with them or why this equals value. It reads like config, not a benefit.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/review/page.tsx lines 141-150 (renders p.text only, ignores p.rationale) + prompt-generator.ts line 63 (rationale exists)
- *Fix:* Show each prompt's customer-safe rationale (already generated) and a plain-language header ("Questions buyers ask AI about you"), so the launch moment communicates value, not raw query config.

**143. ▫︎ If I revisit an onboarding URL after launching, I'm silently bounced with no explanation**
- *Surface:* Onboarding access guard behavior
- *Why it's bad:* requireOnboardingTenant redirects any non-pending tenant away from /onboard/*. So if I bookmark a step, hit back after launching, or want to re-edit my cities, I get silently redirected with no "you've already launched, edit this in Settings" message. Silent redirects make me think I clicked wrong or the app is glitching, and there's no breadcrumb to where I can actually change these inputs post-launch.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/onboard/business/page.tsx line 28 + scope/competitors/review pages (all call requireOnboardingTenant which redirects silently)
- *Fix:* When redirecting a launched tenant off /onboard/*, send them to a clear destination with a message and a link to where they can edit business/cities/competitors (Settings → Config). Don't bounce silently.

**144. ▫︎ Onboarding asks for competitor NAMES, not domains — useless for keyword/rank comparison**
- *Surface:* Onboarding (competitors step)
- *Why it's bad:* Per the onboarding trace, Step 3 takes up to 5 competitor company NAMES (placeholder 'Competitor One / Competitor Two'), not domains. SEMrush organic-competitor and keyword-gap analysis is domain-based. So the competitors I hand-enter at onboarding can't be matched to SEMrush rank/keyword data without a domain. I'd expect to enter competitor URLs for ranking comparison; entering names means my competitive keyword analysis starts blind.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: onboarding competitors step (src/app/(shell)/onboard/competitors/) collects company names, not domains, so they can't feed SEMrush keyword-gap/rank comparison
- *Fix:* Accept competitor domains (or both name + domain) in the onboarding competitors step so the entries can be joined to SEMrush/GSC competitor data for keyword-gap and rank comparison.

**145. ▫︎ Local presence renders a full NAP/listing-health scorecard structure for a brand-new tenant with nothing in it**
- *Surface:* /local — first-run with no data
- *Why it's bad:* local/page.tsx unconditionally calls getLocalPresenceSnapshot() and renders the listing-health score (0-100), NAP states, per-source freshness ('Never synced' / 'No imports yet' via lines 47/51) and review sentiment — even when I've imported/synced nothing. A brand-new user sees a serious-looking 'listing health score' computed from an empty config, which is meaningless and potentially alarming (a low score on day zero implies I'm failing at something I haven't even started). There's no honest top-level 'connect a review source to populate this' empty state guarding the whole page.
- *Fix:* Add a page-level empty-state guard: if there are no stored reviews and no NAP fields configured, show a single 'Connect or import a review source to see local presence' card instead of a scorecard full of zeros and 'Never synced' rows that read as failures.

**146. ▫︎ Dedupe is a forced one-pair-at-a-time wizard with no multi-select and no keyboard shortcuts**
- *Surface:* /changes/dedupe
- *Why it's bad:* dedupe-client.tsx steps through pairs strictly sequentially (index state, advance()). There's a 'Dismiss all remaining' escape hatch but no way to select several specific pairs and archive them together, and zero keyboard support (no a=archive, k=keep, s=skip). For clearing a backlog of duplicates fast, I'm clicking through a slideshow.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/dedupe/dedupe-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Add keyboard shortcuts (a/k/s) mapped to the three buttons, and a list mode with checkboxes for bulk archive of obvious duplicates.

**147. ▫︎ Step progress likely conveyed visually with no announced 'Step N of 4' to assistive tech**
- *Surface:* Onboarding wizard stepper
- *Why it's bad:* The onboarding shell renders a 4-dot stepper. If the current/completed steps are dots styled by color/fill only (the common pattern), I have no idea how far through setup I am or which step I'm on. The page titles say 'Set up your business' but the progress indicator itself needs to tell me 'Step 1 of 4, current'. Dots-by-color is a classic SR dead end.
- *Grounding:* src/components/onboard/onboarding-shell.tsx (ABSENCE: 4-dot stepper Business→Service area→Competitors→Review — verify it exposes current step via aria-current/text, not dots alone)
- *Fix:* Render the stepper as an ordered list with visible/visually-hidden 'Step N of 4 — current/completed' text and aria-current="step" on the active dot; never rely on fill color alone.


## Connectors (GSC/GA4/Clarity/Profound/SEMrush/Wix) (70)

**148. ⛔ The 'push edits live to your Wix site' promise actually requires an operator-only page and pasting raw JSON collection mappings**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors (Wix card) + /diagnostics/wix
- *Why it's bad:* Signup promises Beacon 'pushes the eligible edits live to your site.' But the real Wix publishing surface (diagnostics/wix) is gated by canPublishForCurrentTenant() and 404s for a normal customer, and even if I reach it, it demands I paste a JSON array of {dataCollectionId, slugField, urlPrefix, contentFieldRoles} (lines 145-153) and a 'site id (GUID)'. I'm a bakery owner — I don't know what a dataCollectionId or slugField is. The headline feature I bought is functionally inaccessible to me.

- *Fix:* Build a customer-facing, no-JSON Wix publishing flow (auto-discover collections, friendly field pickers) reachable from /settings/connectors, and surface 'Approve & Push' on /recommendations itself — not behind an operator 404.

**158. ⛔ Every field edit is a full-item REPLACE — a stale read or concurrency means my other fields get silently overwritten**
- *Surface:* Wix update adapter (full-item PUT)
- *Why it's bad:* wixUpdateDataItem (client.ts:211) documents that Wix Update is a full-item PUT that CLEARS any omitted field, so it re-fetches the item and merges. The code comment even admits this exact mechanism already 'blanked koobideh-kabob's slug → a live 404 on 2026-06-13.' The protection is a read-modify-write that depends on the read being fresh and nothing else editing the item between read and write. If I'm also editing in the Wix dashboard, or the read is stale, Beacon's PUT can stomp my concurrent changes across the WHOLE item — not just the one field. For someone terrified of breaking his site, a 'we replace the entire record and hope the merge is right' write model is terrifying, and it has already caused a live 404 once.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/connectors/wix/client.ts
- *Fix:* Use a field-level / partial update API where Wix supports it, add optimistic-concurrency (revision/version check) so a changed-since-read item is refused instead of overwritten, and surface a clear warning that editing in Wix simultaneously is unsafe.

**168. ⛔ The shared FormField primitive disconnects label from input for every sheet**
- ✅ FIXED (2026-06-15, src/components/forms/form-controls.tsx): FormField now mints a stable `useId()` and shares it via context; FormInput/FormSelect/FormTextarea adopt it (`id`) while the label gets `htmlFor`, so label↔control association holds across create-brief / log-change / record-result / lifecycle-actions sheets. Required marker is no longer color-only — `aria-required` + visually-hidden "(required)". Tests: src/components/forms/form-controls.test.tsx.
- *Surface:* Shared form sheets (Log change / Record result / Create brief)
- *Why it's bad:* FormField/FormInput/FormSelect/FormTextarea are the building blocks of every modal sheet in the product. The <label> has no htmlFor and FormInput passes no id, so the association is structurally impossible no matter how callers use them. That means create-brief-sheet, log-change-sheet, record-result-sheet all have orphaned labels. The required asterisk is also color-only (text-status-danger '*') with no 'required' text. Fix the primitive once and every sheet improves.
- *Grounding:* src/components/forms/form-controls.tsx (FormField line 16 bare <label>, no htmlFor; FormInput line 26 no id)
- *Fix:* Generate a stable id in FormField (useId), pass it to children via context or a render prop, set htmlFor; add aria-required and visually-hidden '(required)' text alongside the asterisk.

- *Surface:* /settings/connectors (SEMrush, Profound, Clarity, Wix, GA4 cards)
- *Why it's bad:* Only the GSC card (line 570, and it's behind GBP_AFFORDANCES_ENABLED=false so it never renders) and Yelp (line 893) display 'Last synced'. SEMrush/Profound/Clarity/Wix/GA4 connected cards show only 'Authorized {date}', i.e. when I pasted the key — not when data last came in. For a set-and-forget user this is the exact thing I need to know and it's missing everywhere.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Render last_synced_at (with relative age) on every connected card, not just Yelp/GSC, and color it stale past a threshold.

**178. ⚠️ SEMrush can be connected in two different places that write the same token — 'is it connected?' becomes genuinely ambiguous**

**180. ⚠️ The connectors copy promises 'every edit goes through Approve & Push (your click)' but that click doesn't exist on any page I can reach**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors (Wix card)
- *Why it's bad:* connectors-client.tsx:972 reassures me 'every edit still goes through Approve & Push (your click, daily caps, non-destructive guard)' and line 1041 says 'Used only when you click Approve & Push on an edit.' But as the account owner I have no Approve & Push button anywhere in the normal product — it's only on the 404-for-me /diagnostics/wix page. The reassurance I'm reading at connect time describes a control I can never actually use. That's a broken promise about the single most important safety gate.

**181. ⚠️ 'Disconnecting stops all publishing instantly' — but it also strips my ability to ever Revert prior pushes**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors (Wix disconnect)
- *Why it's bad:* The Wix card promises 'disconnecting stops all publishing instantly' (connectors-client.tsx:1043). True — but Revert ALSO ships through executePush, which needs a live Wix connection (revertPushFromForm → executePush → wixUpdateDataItem). So the moment I panic-disconnect after a bad push, I also lose the ability to roll that bad push back through Beacon. The 'safe off switch' and the 'undo' are coupled: hitting the kill switch traps the very change I wanted to undo. Nobody tells me this.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/connectors/connectors-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Allow (or clearly explain) that Revert requires reconnection, and ideally keep a 'rollback even after disconnect' path; at minimum warn at disconnect time if there are recent pushes that still have un-used snapshots.

**182. ⚠️ In a non-operator deploy, publishing requires the 'owner' role — a normal connected user gets a flat 'not_operator' and no explanation**
- *Surface:* Publish authority (owner role)
- *Why it's bad:* canPublishForCurrentTenant (can-publish.ts) only allows publishing if BEACON_OPERATOR_MODE is on OR the user is an 'owner' member (PUBLISHING_ROLES = {owner}). approveAndPushRecommendedEdit returns {ok:false, reason:'not_operator'} otherwise (recommendations/actions.ts:826). So if I'm signed in but my membership row isn't exactly 'owner', I connected Wix, reviewed edits, click publish, and get 'not_operator' — a confusing accusation that I'm not allowed to change my own site, with no path to fix it.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/auth/can-publish.ts
- *Fix:* Give a clear role-aware message ('Only the account owner can publish to Wix — ask <owner> or switch accounts') and ensure the person who connected Wix is granted the publishing role, so the connect→publish path doesn't dead-end on authorization.

**183. ⚠️ I'm told URLs can never change, but the code's own comments record that a push already blanked a live slug into a 404**
- *Surface:* Slug/URL protection (trust vs prior incident)
- *Why it's bad:* caps.ts and client.ts repeatedly assert URL/slug changes are 'structurally impossible.' Yet client.ts:65-66 and :201-207 document that a slug-stripped payload 'once blanked a live slug → a live 404' and 'blanked koobideh-kabob's slug → a live 404 on 2026-06-13.' The protection now is a re-fetch-and-merge plus an isProtectedUrlField string check (client.ts:67) — heuristic guards bolted on after a real incident. As the owner reading this, the 'impossible' guarantee is undermined by the system's own scar tissue: it HAS broken a live URL, and the fix is pattern-matching that could miss an oddly-named slug field.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/connectors/wix/client.ts
- *Fix:* Don't claim 'structurally impossible'; back URL safety with an explicit allowlist of writable fields per collection (operator-confirmed) and a hard refusal for anything not on it, plus a post-write check that the page still resolves (no new 404) with auto-revert if it doesn't.

**190. ⚠️ Wix card NEVER shows last-synced or last-push — connected forever with zero activity feedback**
- *Surface:* /settings/connectors (Wix card)
- *Why it's bad:* The Wix card only renders 'Connected · Authorized <date>'. There's no last-push time, no push count, no 'last edit went live at X', no error from a failed push. Wix is the one connector that WRITES to my live site, and it's the most opaque card of all. When a push fails on the back end (recorded only in the push ledger, which I can't see), the card stays cheerfully 'Connected.' I have no way to know my edits aren't landing.

**191. ⚠️ A timeout or network blip mid-write isn't retried and surfaces as a flat 'api_error' — and I can't tell if the write half-landed**
- *Surface:* Wix push (network/timeout during write)
- *Why it's bad:* In wix/client.ts wixFetch, the catch block (lines 132-141) explicitly does NOT retry network/timeout errors — only HTTP 429/502/503 are retried. So a 20s timeout (TIMEOUT_MS) on a CMS PUT returns {ok:false, reason:'api_error', detail:<message>} with no retry. Worse: a PUT that timed out may have actually applied on Wix's side, but Beacon records push_failed. Next time I 'retry' I might double-apply or get confused about whether my live site changed. For someone whose whole worry is 'Wix push fails,' silent timeout with no retry and ambiguous state is exactly the trap.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/connectors/wix/client.ts
- *Fix:* Retry idempotent reads on network errors with backoff; for the write PUT, after a timeout do a verify-read of the item before reporting push_failed so the result reflects whether the change actually landed.

**192. ⚠️ The 'Import your data' empty state contradicts the whole self-serve connect flow — it only points to CSV import**

But I'm a self-serve user who connected GSC/GA4/Wix/SEMrush/Profound/Clarity — I never wanted to hand-import a spreadsheet. The empty state silently assumes the old import-a-CSV workflow and never mentions connecting tools. So the dashboard tells me to do the one thing I'm specifically trying to avoid, and hides the path (connectors) I actually need. It makes the product feel like it wasn't built for the way it's being sold.
- *Fix:* Rewrite the empty-state to offer BOTH paths and lead with connectors: 'Connect Google Search Console to see your command center → [Connect tools]' with CSV import as the secondary option.

So once I accept a recommendation, its status pill ('Accepted') renders with no background and default-ish text — and the pill is purely visual anyway (no aria). Accepting an edit is the main action on this page; I get no reliable confirmation that the card flipped to Accepted, by color OR by announcement.
