**472. ⚠️ It admits it only surfaces Perplexity + ChatGPT but never tells me Gemini/Google AI is excluded from my view**
- *Surface:* /prompts (At-a-glance footer)
- *Why it's bad:* AtGlance footer (prompts/page.tsx line 236) says 'Thresholds fixed in v1 — surface only Perplexity + ChatGPT data.' Buried in 11px gray micro-copy. For an AEO buyer this is the single most important caveat — my standings exclude two engines I care about — yet it's whispered at the bottom of a stats strip in the smallest font on the page. Worse, the platform label map includes 'google_aio: Google AI' (page.tsx line 599) so the product half-pretends to support it.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Promote the platform-coverage statement to a clear banner near the top of /prompts: 'Today Beacon reads ChatGPT + Perplexity. Gemini and Google AI Overviews are not yet tracked.' Make it impossible to miss.

**473. ⚠️ Half my answer evidence shows 'Answer text not available' because answer bodies are fetched in a separate fragile query**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] (raw evidence)
- *Why it's bad:* page.tsx lines 155-179 fetch answer_texts in a separate Supabase .in() query inside a try/catch that silently swallows errors (console.error only). If that query fails or a row lacks a body, RawEvidence renders 'Answer text not available for this observation.' (page.tsx line 891). For an AEO user the raw answer text is the WHOLE point — I need to read exactly how ChatGPT described my competitor instead of me. Getting an italic 'not available' shrug on the most important evidence is maddening, and it fails quietly so I never know why.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Store answer body alongside the observation (or join it server-side reliably) and surface a real error/retry state instead of a silent 'not available' when the fetch fails.

**474. ⚠️ Two completely different Prompts pages ship behind an invisible flag; which one I see is luck**
- *Surface:* /prompts (V2 vs legacy split)
- *Why it's bad:* prompts/page.tsx lines 35-41: default = legacy decision view; ?v2=1 = a totally different 'strategic surface' (PromptsV2Client) with different labels ('Almost there', 'Missing', 'Still learning') and different layout; BEACON_PROMPTS_V2 env flips the default but is 'NOT flipped yet'. The legacy footer even hides the legacy-escape link 'because v2 is the production default and the visible link made the product feel unfinished' (prompts-v2-client.tsx lines 97-104) — but the code default is STILL legacy. So the comments contradict the code, and I have no idea whether I'm looking at the real product or a preview. Two AEO vocabularies for the same data is confusing as a daily user.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx + src/app/(shell)/prompts/prompts-v2-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Pick one Prompts surface, delete the other, and remove the env/query flag. Stop shipping two divergent AEO vocabularies.

**475. ⚠️ 'Words AI used near you' depends on descriptor_window which Profound/legacy rows don't populate — so it's often falsely empty**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] (descriptor cloud) + classifier
- *Why it's bad:* DescriptorCloud (page.tsx lines 773-810) reads drilldown.descriptorsNearBrand, sourced from o.descriptor_window (opportunity-classify.ts line 228). For an AEO user this chip cloud — the actual adjectives ChatGPT attaches to me vs competitors — is gold. But descriptor_window is a native-extraction field; legacy/Profound rows leave it empty, so the page shows 'AI hasn't described you on this prompt yet' even when there ARE answers that mention me. I'd conclude AI says nothing about me when really the field just wasn't captured.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Disclose when the descriptor data is unavailable due to source (vs genuinely absent), and extract descriptors from stored answer_texts where available rather than only the native descriptor_window.

**476. ⚠️ Header promises prompts 'run in tomorrow's daily AI check' — there is no daily check**
- *Surface:* /settings/prompts (header copy)
- *Why it's bad:* settings/prompts/page.tsx line 94: 'X prompts run in tomorrow's daily AI check.' Same false-automation lie as /prompts, but here it's on the exact page where I manage my prompt set, so it's the most damaging place to be told 'tomorrow it'll auto-run.' I'd toggle on 20 prompts, close the tab, and expect data tomorrow morning that never arrives because crons are off.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/prompts/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Change to honest copy reflecting on-demand polling, e.g. 'X prompts will be included the next time you run an AI reading.'

**477. ⚠️ Google AI Overviews is silently locked to null with no explanation to me**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] (Google AI Overviews handling)
- *Why it's bad:* prompt-detail-v2-client.tsx lines 378-379: 'Other platforms (e.g., google_aio) intentionally return null because Section 6 D6 locks Google AI [Overviews].' And the observation type comment (types.ts lines 20-21) admits 'Google AI Overview never exposes [search queries] — accepted blind spot.' So even where Google AI appears as a label, its primary-share is hardcoded null and the limitation is documented only in code comments. As an AEO user I'm never told that a whole engine's data is structurally unavailable; it just shows blanks.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/prompt-detail-v2-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Surface an explicit per-platform 'not measurable' state in the UI for Google AI Overviews instead of silent nulls, so I understand it's a known limitation, not missing data.

**478. ⚠️ AEO is front-and-center in nav while Google ranking work is buried — the product is upside-down for an SEO**
- *Surface:* /prompts framing
- *Why it's bad:* The prompts page is literally titled 'AI answers (optional)' yet 'Prompts' gets a permanent top-level nav slot (navigation.ts line 33) while /topics (keyword opportunities) and /pages (per-URL Google data) are hidden/dead. So the optional AEO surface is promoted and the core Google surfaces are demoted or removed. For someone who cares about impressions/CTR/position, the information architecture actively steers me away from what I bought it for.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/lib/navigation.ts
- *Fix:* Rebalance nav: surface a Google-rankings/Opportunities entry at the same level as Prompts. If AEO is 'optional', it shouldn't outrank the keyword tooling in the sidebar.

**479. ⚠️ The hidden 'Opportunities' page is built around AI citation events, not keyword/search opportunities**
- *Surface:* /topics content (Opportunities)
- *Why it's bad:* Even if I find /topics, its rows are derived from outcome EVENTS (AI visibility shifts), platform breakdowns, and 'Review decisions' (topics/page.tsx builds eventsByTopic from detectOutcomeEvents and statuses like 'breakthrough/unresolved'). The page header sells 'Google Search demand, page scans, and AI citations' but the status engine and next-moves are about AI-answer events and a 'Review' queue, not keyword volume, ranking position, or striking distance. A traditional SEO opening 'Opportunities' finds an AEO attribution workflow, not a keyword opportunity list.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/topics/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a keyword-first opportunity mode to /topics driven by gsc_page_signals + SEMrush gaps (volume, position, CTR potential), distinct from the AI-event attribution view.

**480. ⚠️ Prompts page promises 'Beacon checks AI visibility every morning' — another automation lie to a new user**
- *Surface:* /prompts — too-early empty state
- *Why it's bad:* With prompts active but no readings, /prompts (page.tsx line 158) shows: 'X prompts active but no AI readings in the last 7 days. Beacon checks AI visibility every morning.' Same crons-off problem: it asserts a daily automatic check that doesn't happen. I'd read this as 'great, it's running, just wait' — and it never runs. The AtGlance footer (line 234) compounds it: 'Lookback: last 7 days of AI readings... Beacon checks AI visibility every morning.' Every one of these surfaces trains me to expect autopilot that doesn't exist.
- *Fix:* Replace 'Beacon checks AI visibility every morning' with the honest on-demand reality and an action: 'No readings yet. [Run an AI reading now]'. Audit the whole codebase for 'every morning'/'daily check'/'tomorrow morning' strings and make them consistent with the methodology FAQ's 'no background schedule'.

**481. ⚠️ Toggling ONE prompt locks the ENTIRE list — every button shares disabled={pending}**
- *Surface:* /settings/prompts
- *Why it's bad:* settings-prompts-client.tsx wires every row's Activate/Deactivate button to disabled={pending} from a single useTransition (lines 27, 234-236). When I toggle prompt #1, every other toggle in the list greys out until the round-trip finishes. As a power user batch-toggling 20 prompts I can only do them strictly serially, watching the whole UI freeze between each click. There's also no select-all / bulk-toggle.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/prompts/settings-prompts-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Track pending state per-row (Set of in-flight ids) so toggles are independent and optimistic. Add 'Activate all / Deactivate all' and a multi-select.

**482. ⚠️ No keyboard nav, no bulk select, no search on the prompt manager — pure mouse, one toggle per click**
- *Surface:* /settings/prompts
- *Why it's bad:* This is a list I curate constantly (which prompts run tomorrow). settings-prompts-client.tsx renders a plain <ul> with one toggle button each, no checkboxes, no select-all, no j/k, no filter box. With dozens of prompts, turning off a batch (say all the cost-question ones) means hunting and clicking each individually. The prompt is literally about queue/list management speed and it's the slowest possible UI.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/prompts/settings-prompts-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Add multi-select with a bulk Activate/Deactivate bar, a search/filter input, and j/k row focus + space-to-toggle.

**483. ⚠️ Prompts page splits my world into FIVE colored buckets I must mentally rank**
- *Surface:* /prompts (default legacy)
- *Why it's bad:* I open Prompts hoping for clarity and get 5 simultaneous category sections — Outranked (red), Absent (orange), Close (yellow), Winning (green), Early (gray) — each with its own count and lead line (prompts/page.tsx:263-308). There's no 'here are the 3 that matter most' — every bucket competes for attention with traffic-light colors. Five priority colors on one page is decision paralysis by design.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx:263-308 (CATEGORY_META 5 buckets) + AtGlance:188-245
- *Fix:* Lead with a single 'Top 3 prompts to fix' list; collapse the 5-bucket breakdown into an expandable 'see all' below.

**484. ▫︎ The Prompts page is titled 'AI answers (optional)' and buried in jargon like 'Outranked', 'Absent', 'AI-answer intelligence'**
- *Surface:* /prompts
- *Why it's bad:* Header (line 145-147): 'Optional AI-answer intelligence — how AI assistants answer your buyers' questions.' Category labels (lines 273-307): Outranked / Absent / Close / Winning, plus a 'Not on the list' pill. As a bakery owner I have no mental model for 'AI-answer intelligence' or being 'Outranked' in an AI answer. Calling a whole tab '(optional)' also tells me it's not worth my time, so I'll ignore data I'm paying for.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Rename to something concrete ('Questions customers ask AI') and use plain status words: 'They recommend a competitor instead of you' rather than 'Outranked'. Drop the '(optional)' tag or explain the value.

**485. ▫︎ Add-prompt form demands a 'Topic id' field with cryptic '[city] [service]' placeholder — jargon I can't fill on a phone**
- *Surface:* /prompts add-prompt placeholder copy — settings-prompts-client.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* The add-prompt form (lines 128-136) has a REQUIRED 'Topic id' input with placeholder '[city] [service]' and a 'Location (optional)' field. As a shop owner I have no idea what a 'topic id' is or what bracket syntax it wants. Required jargon fields block me from adding the one prompt I actually care about, and on a phone the tiny labels + bracket placeholders give me nothing to work with.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/prompts/settings-prompts-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Drop or auto-derive 'topic id' for customers, or relabel it in plain English with a real example; never require an internal id field from a non-technical mobile user.

**486. ▫︎ My personal daily cap silently falls back to a hardcoded $10 if my tenant row lacks the field — and I'm never told which applies**
- *Surface:* Per-tenant daily budget ($10) source of truth
- *Why it's bad:* resolveTenantDailyBudgetUsd reads tenant.daily_budget_usd and falls back to GLOBAL_DEFAULT_DAILY_BUDGET_USD = 10 on any miss or error. So my effective daily ceiling depends on whether an invisible DB column is set — and there's no UI showing me whether I'm on a custom cap or the $10 default. I can't tell what my own limit is. For budgeting, 'your cap is some number we won't show you, possibly a fallback' is unacceptable.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/observations/run-poll.ts lines 54-63 (GLOBAL_DEFAULT_DAILY_BUDGET_USD = 10 fallback on miss/error)
- *Fix:* Surface the resolved daily cap in the spend UI with its source ('Your daily limit: $10 (default)') and let me change it.

**487. ▫︎ Per-platform standing dumps a comma-soup of internal counts when I'm not clearly winning or absent**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] (PlatformSplit fallback)
- *Why it's bad:* page.tsx lines 651-654: the fallback line reads 'Primary 2 of 7 · cited 1 · absent 4' / 'Cited in 1 of 7 (avg #3)' / 'Mentioned in 2 of 7, never cited'. For the most common middle states this is a string of raw tallies, not a decision. I want 'ChatGPT mentions you but ranks competitor X above you' — instead I parse arithmetic. The clean copy only exists for the binary winning/absent extremes.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Write plain-English per-platform verdicts for the middle states (cited-not-primary, mentioned-not-cited) instead of concatenating primary/cited/absent counts.

**488. ▫︎ The list and the detail page use different category names for the same state**
- *Surface:* /prompts (category labels) vs /prompts/[id]
- *Why it's bad:* The v2 list (v2-projection.ts lines 74-110) labels states 'Winning / Almost there / Missing / Outranked / Still learning'. The legacy detail page (prompts/[id]/page.tsx CATEGORY_META lines 363-402) labels them 'Outranked / Absent / Close / Winning / Early'. So a prompt shown as 'Missing' in the list becomes 'Absent' on its own detail page, and 'Almost there' becomes 'Close', and 'Still learning' becomes 'Early'. Clicking a row changes the vocabulary on me mid-flow.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/prompts/v2-projection.ts + src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Unify the category vocabulary across the list and detail surfaces (one projection table feeding both).

**489. ▫︎ It tells me the dominant answer shape only when ONE structure hits 60%+, so mixed-format prompts get no guidance**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] ('Answer shape' advice)
- *Why it's bad:* page.tsx lines 293-296 + drilldown logic (prompt-drilldown.ts lines 199-208): AnswerShapeCallout only renders when a single structure has >=60% share. For the very common case where ChatGPT answers a question as a ranked list 40% of the time and a paragraph 40%, I get NOTHING — no hint about how to format my content to be extractable. The most strategically useful AEO advice (match the answer shape) is suppressed exactly when the field is contested.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Show the top 2 answer shapes with their shares when no single one dominates, so I always get formatting guidance.

**490. ▫︎ Drilldown header says it's built for 'Ritz' and assumes a builder mental model in its own doc comment**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] (back link)
- *Why it's bad:* prompts/[id]/page.tsx lines 60-64 describe the layout as 'Platform split: 2 cards, Ritz's state per platform' and '4. Words AI used near you … (when Ritz mentioned).' The page's design contract is written around Ritz Builders. While in code comments, it reflects that the AEO drilldown's logic and copy ('worth appearing in one', 'enter the field') were tuned for a local-services builder, not an AEO-first brand competing for informational queries in ChatGPT — the framing assumes geo/local intent I may not have.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Audit the drilldown copy and logic for builder/local-services assumptions (geo clusters, 'enter the field') and make them conditional on tenant intent type.

**491. ▫︎ Prompt rows show cryptic 'geo · …' / 'topic · …' cluster pills and a 'Not on the list' tag with no explanation**
- *Surface:* /prompts (cluster pills)
- *Why it's bad:* prompts/page.tsx PromptRow (lines 396-419) renders cluster tag pills ('geo · Cupertino, CA', 'topic · …') and a 'Not on the list' pill for ranked_list_miss. There's no tooltip or legend. As an AEO user scanning my prompts I see decorative-looking tags whose meaning ('this question is answered as a ranked list and you're not on it') is exactly the insight I need — but it's compressed into a 10px pill labeled 'Not on the list' with zero context.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Add hover/explainer text to the cluster and ranked_list_miss pills, or expand 'Not on the list' to 'AI answers this as a ranked list — you're not on it.'

**492. ▫︎ The 'Too early' empty state says '7 days' but the page only loaded 14 days and the classifier filters to the native regime start**
- *Surface:* /prompts (early-state copy mismatch)
- *Why it's bad:* prompts/page.tsx line 158 hardcodes 'no AI readings in the last 7 days' in the empty state, while the data fetch uses 14 days (line 88) and the classifier clamps the window to NATIVE_REGIME_START (opportunity-classify.ts lines 142-145). So the number I'm told (7 days) doesn't match what was actually queried (14) or evaluated (native-regime-clamped). For someone trying to understand why I have no AI data, three inconsistent windows is confusing and erodes trust in the numbers.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Make the empty-state window text reflect the actual lookback used, and keep the fetch/classifier/copy windows consistent.

**493. ▫︎ When no one dominates, the verdict is a math riddle: 'No single primary — 3 different entities split the top slot'**
- *Surface:* /prompts/[id] ('Who IS the answer' wording)
- *Why it's bad:* prompts/[id]/page.tsx lines 687-691: the fragmented case renders 'No single primary — N different entities split the top slot across M answers.' That's the most strategically important AEO situation for me — the answer is up for grabs and I could WIN it — but it's phrased as a neutral statistics observation, not an opportunity. It doesn't tell me 'this is winnable; here's how.' The framing buries my best opening.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/[id]/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Reframe the fragmented/no-clear-winner state as an opportunity with a next step ('No one owns this answer yet — strong chance to become the default if you publish a clean answer block').

**494. ▫︎ It tells me thresholds are 'fixed in v1' so I can't tune what counts as Winning/Close/Outranked for my brand**
- *Surface:* /prompts (At-a-glance threshold caveat)
- *Why it's bad:* prompts/page.tsx line 236: 'Thresholds fixed in v1.' The classifier hardcodes winningPrimaryRate 0.5, minObservations 3, outrankedMinCompetitors 2 (opportunity-classify.ts lines 95-101). For my AEO use case 'primary in 50% of answers = Winning' may be far too lax (I might want 'primary in 80%+' before calling it won), but the product tells me I can't change it. The categorization that drives my whole daily decision is a black box I can't calibrate.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Expose the key classification thresholds as tenant settings (or at least document the exact thresholds in plain English so I know what 'Winning' means).

**495. ▫︎ Adding a prompt requires a mouse-opened form with a 'topic id' free-text field and no keyboard shortcut to add**
- *Surface:* Prompts list — add form
- *Why it's bad:* settings-prompts-client.tsx 'Add prompt' is a mouse-click to reveal a form (lines 96-103) requiring a 'Topic id' text field with a cryptic placeholder '[city] [service]' (lines 124-135). For a power user curating prompts in bulk, there's no 'press n to add', no inline quick-add, and the required topic-id field is jargon I have to guess at. Slow and friction-heavy for what should be rapid list editing.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/prompts/settings-prompts-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Add an inline quick-add (press n, type prompt, Enter) that auto-derives topic, and make topic-id optional/auto.

**496. ▫︎ Prompts is labeled 'AI answers (optional)' — so why is it in my main nav?**
- *Surface:* /prompts (title/copy)
- *Why it's bad:* The page header literally says 'AI answers (optional)' and 'Your core search-demand moves live on Today and Recommendations' (prompts/page.tsx:146), yet Prompts is one of only 5 top-level nav items. Putting an explicitly-optional surface in my primary navigation adds a tab I have to evaluate-and-dismiss every visit. More cards/tabs to triage = more freeze.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/prompts/page.tsx:145-147 (title 'AI answers (optional)') + src/lib/navigation.ts:33
- *Fix:* If it's optional, move Prompts under Settings or behind a 'More' affordance — keep the primary nav to the essentials I act on daily.


## SEO / GSC / keywords (4)

**497. ⛔ /topics ("Opportunities" — keyword/striking-distance view) is hidden from the entire nav**
- *Surface:* Navigation / /topics
- *Why it's bad:* I bought this to work keyword opportunities and striking-distance. The one surface that ranks topics and 'where to win next' (/topics, titled 'Opportunities') is deliberately removed from the sidebar — src/lib/navigation.ts line 21: 'Hidden from nav 2026-04-17 ... /topics'. The nav is only Today / Recommendations / Prompts / Changes / Settings. I literally cannot find the opportunity view without someone telling me to type the URL. For a set-and-forget user that means it never gets used.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/lib/navigation.ts
- *Fix:* Re-add /topics to navigationGroups in src/lib/navigation.ts with a clear label like 'Opportunities'. If it's hidden because it reads stale stores, fix the data path rather than hiding the page; a keyword tool with no opportunities tab is broken by definition.

**498. ⚠️ No browsable list of the keywords/queries I rank for — GSC queries only appear one-at-a-time inside rec cards**
- *Surface:* Keyword inventory / query list
- *Why it's bad:* GscQuerySignal carries top queries per page (capped at 8) but the only place a query surfaces to me is as the single 'worst' query inside one rec card's prose. There is no screen where I can browse all my ranking queries, sort by impressions/position, or filter by striking distance. Every SEO tool I've used opens to a query/keyword table; here the keyword data is locked inside individual recommendation cards and capped, so I can never see my full keyword footprint.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no customer-facing keyword/query inventory table; GscQuerySignal.topQueries (gsc-page-signals.ts, capped at 8) is only ever exposed as the single worst query inside a rec card
- *Fix:* Add a Queries/Keywords table sourced from gsc-page-signals topQueries (and SEMrush keywords) with sort/filter by impressions, clicks, position, CTR. This is table-stakes for the persona.

**499. ⚠️ Live routes show 'Coming in CX3' placeholder boxes — a new user sees unfinished scaffolding presented as real pages**
- *Surface:* /audit, /rank, /moves/[id] (builder stubs)
- *Why it's bad:* audit/page.tsx renders 'Your AI Visibility Audit' with a dashed box reading 'Coming in CX3 — audit summary with paste-ready moves'. /rank and /moves/[id] are the same. These are live, reachable URLs. As a brand-new user exploring my shiny new tool, stumbling onto 'Coming in CX3' (an internal sprint codename that means nothing to me) makes the product feel like a half-built prototype. 'CX3' is exactly the kind of internal jargon a paying customer should never see.
- *Fix:* Either remove these routes entirely (return notFound) until they're real, or replace the CX-codename placeholders with honest, customer-facing copy ('This view isn't available yet') with a link to a working surface — never an internal sprint label.

**500. ▫︎ j/k 'Enter to open' navigates via window.location.href — a full hard page reload instead of client routing**
- *Surface:* Topics / Pages keyboard nav
- *Why it's bad:* pages-client.tsx line 229 and topics-client.tsx line 434/links use window.location.href for Enter-to-open, which triggers a full document reload (white flash, re-fetch, lost scroll) instead of Next.js client navigation. For a keyboard user flying through rows with Enter, every open is a jarring full reload. The rest of the app uses <Link>/router.push.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/pages/pages-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Use router.push() (next/navigation) for keyboard Enter navigation to keep it a fast client transition.


## Errors / failures / recovery (5)

**501. ⚠️ Diagnostics can render 'Configuration not loaded — running on neutral placeholder … brand-shaped surfaces render empty across the app'**
- *Surface:* /diagnostics (placeholder-config banner)
- *Why it's bad:* The diagnostics page shows a warning that the whole app may be running on an empty placeholder config when no tenant config resolves. If I, as the owner, ever hit this state, it means every brand/domain/competitor-shaped number I've been reading could be blank or wrong app-wide — and I'd only discover it on an operator page I'm not supposed to see. The skeptic's nightmare: the numbers were never anchored to my business and nothing on the customer surface said so.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/diagnostics/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Promote a non-scary but visible signal to the customer surface when the resolved config is a placeholder (e.g. a 'finish setup' banner on /), so an unconfigured tenant never silently renders empty/neutral numbers that look real.

**502. ⚠️ Login error and 'check your email' success are never announced**
- *Surface:* /login
- *Why it's bad:* When my magic-link request fails, the error <p> just appears silently — no role="alert". When it succeeds, the whole form is replaced by a 'Check your email' box with no aria-live, so my screen reader stays quiet and I think nothing happened and click again. Both the failure and success paths leave me blind at the most critical moment.
- *Grounding:* src/app/login/login-form.tsx (error <p> line 51 no role; success swap line 30-36 no live region)
- *Fix:* Add role="alert" to the error paragraph and role="status" (or move focus to) the success box.

**503. ▫︎ If nothing shipped while I was gone, /changes can't distinguish 'quiet 2 weeks' from 'broken'**
- *Surface:* /changes empty state
- *Why it's bad:* changes/page.tsx lines 151-182 shows one flat 'No changes yet' empty state whenever there are no live entries + no recommended edits. As a returning user that's ambiguous and alarming: did genuinely nothing happen in 14 days (plausible, since autopilot is off and I shipped nothing), or did something break and lose my history? The empty copy ('Accept a recommendation to get your first change tracked') reads like a brand-new tenant, not like a returning user whose 2 weeks were simply quiet. I can't tell if the silence is real or a bug.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Differentiate the returning-user empty state ('No changes since your last visit on {date} — your queue and data are intact') from the brand-new 'No changes yet,' using the server-side last-seen marker.

**504. ▫︎ Proposed Briefs empty state demands 'your standard .xlsx export' — assumes I'm a returning power user, not brand-new**
- *Surface:* /briefs/proposed — empty state
- *Why it's bad:* briefs/proposed/page.tsx line 33: 'Import visibility data (e.g. your standard .xlsx export) to generate execution briefs from pattern intelligence.' 'Your standard .xlsx export' presumes I already have an established export workflow from some prior tool. I'm brand-new — I have no 'standard export', I don't know what columns it needs, and 'execution briefs from pattern intelligence' is pure jargon. This empty state speaks to a migrating Profound power-user, not a first-run user, leaving me with no idea what file to produce or why.
- *Fix:* Rewrite for the zero-state user: explain what a brief is in one plain sentence, and offer the connect-a-tool path first. If .xlsx import is supported, link to a template/spec so 'standard export' isn't an undefined assumption.

**505. ▫︎ Palette silently caps changelog results to last 50 and 25 per group while searching — my search can miss real items with no indication**
- *Surface:* ⌘K palette result cap
- *Why it's bad:* layout.tsx slices changelogEntries to the last 50 (CHANGELOG_PALETTE_CAP, lines 44/177-180) BEFORE the client even filters, and command-palette.tsx caps maxPerGroup at 25 when searching (line 115). So if the change I'm hunting is the 51st-newest, ⌘K can't find it and just shows 'No results' — making me think it doesn't exist. Silent truncation is worse than a slow full search for someone who trusts the palette as source of truth.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/layout.tsx
- *Fix:* Search the full changelog server-side (or paginate results), and if truncated, show a 'showing first N — refine your search' note instead of pretending there's nothing.


## Copy / jargon / tone (5)

**506. ⛔ The entire Business Configuration form is unlabeled — every field is 'edit text' to me**
- *Surface:* /settings/config
- *Why it's bad:* This is where I set up my whole business profile — name, domain, phone, address, industry, service areas, services, competitors, flagged terms. I verified zero htmlFor in this file. So my screen reader announces every input as a generic 'edit text' with no name. Filling out my own business profile becomes an unwinnable guessing game of counting fields, and a wrong industry/domain quietly poisons every recommendation for 6 months.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/settings/config/config-form.tsx (labels at lines 114,123,133,143,168,185,195,205... all bare <label> with no htmlFor; inputs have no id)
- *Fix:* Add id to each input/select/textarea and htmlFor to each label. This is the single highest-impact accessibility fix in Settings.

**507. ⛔ The email field at the front door has no associated label**
- *Surface:* /login
- *Why it's bad:* This is the very first interaction with the product. The 'Email' label is a detached <label>, the input relies on a placeholder ('you@example.com'). My screen reader lands on autoFocus'd 'edit text, you@example.com' — placeholder-as-label, which disappears the moment I type. If I can't confidently identify the login field, I can't even get in.
- *Grounding:* src/app/login/login-form.tsx (line 40 <label> with no htmlFor; line 41 <input> with no id)
- *Fix:* Add id="email" to the input and htmlFor="email" to the label. Trivial, but it's the door.

**508. ⛔ The command palette is a modal with no dialog role, no focus trap, and no labeled search**
- *Surface:* Command palette (⌘K, global)
- *Why it's bad:* ⌘K is pushed as the primary navigation affordance (it's in the header hint and every nav shortcut). But the modal has no role="dialog", no aria-modal="true", no focus trap — so after it opens, Tab walks me right out into the page behind the backdrop while the visual overlay is still up. There's no aria-label on the search input either. For me this is a maze: I'm 'in' a dialog the screen reader doesn't know is a dialog, navigating items it can't frame.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/command-palette.tsx (overlay line 158; no role="dialog"/aria-modal; input line 169 no aria-label)
- *Fix:* Add role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Command palette" to the panel, trap focus within it, aria-label the input, and restore focus to the trigger on close.

**509. ⚠️ The <main> region and sidebar <nav> have no accessible names**
- *Surface:* Global (every shell page)
- *Why it's bad:* My screen reader's landmark rotor is how I jump around a page without linear tabbing. An unnamed <main> just reads 'main', and the <nav> reads 'navigation' with no qualifier. When a page also has the lifecycle-strip role=region and other regions, I can't tell them apart in the rotor. Multiple unlabeled landmarks are nearly useless.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/layout.tsx (line 203 <main> has no aria-label; src/components/shell/app-sidebar.tsx line 67 <nav> has no aria-label)
- *Fix:* Add aria-label="Page content" to <main> and aria-label="Primary" to the sidebar <nav>; label the MobileSidebar nav distinctly too.

**510. ⚠️ The mobile menu (hamburger) button has no accessible name or expanded state**
- *Surface:* Header (mobile)
- *Why it's bad:* On a narrow viewport the only way to reach navigation is this button, but it's icon-only (<Menu/>) with no aria-label, so my screen reader just says 'button'. It also controls the MobileSidebar sheet but carries no aria-expanded/aria-controls, so I can't tell if the menu is open or closed. The entire site nav hides behind an unnamed button.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/app-header.tsx (lines 41-48: icon-only Button with <Menu/>, no aria-label, no aria-expanded)
- *Fix:* Add aria-label="Open navigation menu", aria-expanded bound to sidebarOpen, and aria-controls referencing the sheet id.


## Mobile / responsive (7)

**511. ⛔ No viewport meta declared at all — zero mobile intent in the root layout**
- *Surface:* global app shell (all routes) — src/app/layout.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* I open this on my phone 100% of the time. The root layout (src/app/layout.tsx) exports only `metadata` with a title/description and NO `viewport` export and no <meta name=viewport>. The entire app was clearly designed and tested at desktop width — every surface I touch (dense tables, 10px pills, 12px checkboxes) is sized for a mouse on a 1120px canvas, and nothing in the codebase signals anyone ever considered a 360px screen. This is the root cause of every other mobile complaint below.
- *Grounding:* src/app/layout.tsx
- *Fix:* Add an explicit Next.js `export const viewport: Viewport = { width: 'device-width', initialScale: 1 }` to src/app/layout.tsx, and adopt a mobile-first pass: bump base font sizes, ensure 44px min touch targets, and add a real responsive review gate to CI so desktop-only sizing can't ship again.

**512. ⛔ Hamburger menu button is 32px (h-8 w-8) — below the 44px touch minimum, and it's the ONLY way to navigate on mobile**
- *Surface:* global header / mobile nav — src/components/shell/app-header.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* On a phone the sidebar is hidden (md:block) and the single hamburger at line 41-48 is `h-8 w-8` (32px) holding a `h-4 w-4` (16px) icon. That 32px tap target is the entire navigation system on mobile. Between customers, half-glancing at my phone, I miss it constantly and have to re-tap. Every screen switch starts with a fat-finger gamble.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/app-header.tsx
- *Fix:* Make the menu trigger at least 44x44px (`h-11 w-11`), increase the icon to h-5 w-5, and increase the header height from h-12 (48px) so the touch zone isn't cramped against the top edge.

**513. ⚠️ No bottom tab bar or persistent mobile nav — every navigation requires opening the 32px hamburger sheet**
- *Surface:* ABSENCE: mobile bottom nav / persistent navigation
- *Why it's bad:* The mobile nav is exclusively the Sheet that slides from the 32px hamburger (app-sidebar.tsx MobileSidebar). There's no bottom tab bar, no persistent shortcuts. For a phone-first user who bounces between Today, Recommendations, and Changes dozens of times a day between customers, every single jump = tap tiny hamburger, wait for sheet, tap a small nav row, sheet closes. The friction compounds into the app feeling slow and clumsy even when it isn't.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no bottom nav; mobile nav only via Sheet in src/components/shell/app-sidebar.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a mobile bottom tab bar for the 4-5 primary destinations (Today / Recommendations / Prompts / Changes / Settings) with 44px+ touch targets, so the most common navigation is one thumb-reach away.

**514. ⚠️ The shared Button's default and icon sizes top out at 32px (h-8 / size-8) — no touch-sized variant exists**
- *Surface:* global button sizing — src/components/ui/button.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* buttonVariants (lines 22-34) defines default=h-8 (32px), sm=h-7, xs=h-6, icon=size-8 (32px), lg=h-9 (36px). The LARGEST standard button in the design system is 36px — still under the 44px touch minimum, and most usages take the 32px default. So even where the app uses the proper Button component, I'm tapping sub-spec targets. The problem is baked into the primitive every screen inherits.
- *Grounding:* src/components/ui/button.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a touch-sized variant (h-11 / size-11 = 44px) and make it the default on coarse-pointer/mobile, or globally raise the default button height on touch devices.

**515. ▫︎ Content uses p-6 (24px) padding on every side even on mobile, eating ~13% of a 360px screen**
- *Surface:* main content container — src/app/(shell)/layout.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* The main wrapper (line 205) is `mx-auto max-w-[1120px] p-6 lg:p-8`. On a 360px phone, 24px left + 24px right padding leaves only ~312px for content that's already cramped with fixed-width tables and tiny text. There's no `px-3` or `px-4` mobile step-down. Every dense surface loses precious horizontal room it can't spare.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/layout.tsx
- *Fix:* Step the padding down on mobile (e.g. `p-3 sm:p-6 lg:p-8`) so dense content gets the full narrow viewport width.

**516. ▫︎ The header advertises ⌘K / ? keyboard shortcuts (md+) — useless cues for a phone-only user; primary search is keyboard-gated**
- *Surface:* global header — keyboard hints + command palette — src/components/shell/app-header.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* The header shows '⌘K search · ? shortcuts' (lines 67-77) and the whole product leans on a CommandPalette for navigation/search. On a phone I have no ⌘ key and no '?' shortcut. The fast-navigation path the app was built around simply doesn't exist for me, and the only mobile entry is the tiny 32px hamburger. There's no mobile search affordance at all.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/app-header.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a visible, tappable search/command button in the mobile header (not just a keyboard shortcut), and hide desktop-only kbd hints from touch users.

**517. ▫︎ Tenant switch buttons are 12px text with py-0.5 (~18px tall) in the header — sub-thumb-size and crowd the mobile header**
- *Surface:* tenant switcher — src/components/shell/tenant-switcher.tsx
- *Why it's bad:* If I have more than one business, the switcher (lines 56-73) renders `text-[12px]` buttons with `px-2 py-0.5` (~18px tall) in the already-cramped 48px header. Switching which business I'm looking at is a fundamental action, and these are some of the smallest tappable elements in the app, sitting next to the tiny hamburger and any kbd hints.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/tenant-switcher.tsx
- *Fix:* On mobile, move tenant switching into a proper menu/sheet with 44px rows instead of micro text-buttons jammed into the header bar.


## Accessibility (6)

**518. ⚠️ Every page registers its own global window keydown listener with bespoke input-guard logic — they conflict and behave inconsistently**
- *Surface:* Whole app — keyboard handlers
- *Why it's bad:* There's no shared keyboard system (keyboard-shortcut-scope.ts even comments 'no global shortcut system'). command-palette.tsx, review-queue-client.tsx, today-findings.tsx, topics-client.tsx, pages-client.tsx each addEventListener('keydown', ...) on window with slightly different typing-target guards (some check isContentEditable, some don't; some use the shared helper, most don't). On a page that mounts two of these, my keystroke can be handled twice or by the wrong one. As a power user relying on muscle memory, inconsistent key behavior across pages is exhausting and untrustworthy.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no central keyboard shortcut registry — each page hand-rolls window keydown (see src/lib/keyboard-shortcut-scope.ts comment 'no global shortcut system')
- *Fix:* Introduce one keyboard-shortcut provider/registry with consistent scoping and the shared isKeyboardTypingTarget guard everywhere; pages register scoped shortcuts instead of raw window listeners.

**519. ⚠️ Arrow-key result selection is a custom listbox with no ARIA — I can't tell what's selected**
- *Surface:* Command palette (⌘K results)
- *Why it's bad:* Selection moves with Arrow keys and is shown ONLY by a background-color change (bg-accent-primary-muted). There's no role="listbox"/role="option", no aria-selected, no aria-activedescendant. So as I arrow through results my screen reader announces nothing changing — I'm moving an invisible cursor. Color-only selection is exactly what fails me.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/command-palette.tsx (handlePaletteKeyDown line 130; selected item styled by bg only, line 200)
- *Fix:* Make the list role="listbox", items role="option" with aria-selected, and set aria-activedescendant on the input to the selected option id.

**520. ⚠️ Active nav item is shown by color/background only — no aria-current**
- *Surface:* Sidebar nav
- *Why it's bad:* Which page I'm on is conveyed only by a background tint and icon color on the active link. There's no aria-current="page". So as I tab the nav, every item reads identically ('Today, link', 'Recommendations, link') and I cannot tell which one is the page I'm currently viewing — I lose my place constantly.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/app-sidebar.tsx (isActive styling lines 90-95; no aria-current)
- *Fix:* Add aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined} to each nav Link.

**521. ▫︎ No keyboard shortcut to switch tenants or open the tenant switcher — mouse-only on a control I hit constantly**
- *Surface:* Tenant switcher / app header
- *Why it's bad:* app-header.tsx renders the TenantSwitcher in rightSlot with no associated hotkey, and it's not a ⌘K command. For an operator juggling multiple tenants (Ritz / Iranopedia / Finglish per the memory), switching is a frequent action and it's pure mouse. A power user expects something like ⌘K → tenant name, or a dedicated chord.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/app-header.tsx
- *Fix:* Add tenants as ⌘K palette items ('Switch to <tenant>') and/or a hotkey to open the switcher.

**522. ▫︎ '?' help and 'g' nav fire from anywhere on the page, but there's no visible focus ring / state telling me a chord is armed**
- *Surface:* Help modal — '?' opens on real pages but not while typing context is ambiguous
- *Why it's bad:* In command-palette.tsx, pressing 'g' starts a 500ms window for the second key (lines 62-90) with zero on-screen feedback — no 'g…' indicator. As a fast typist I never know if my 'g' registered or timed out, so 'g c' often misfires into nothing when I'm slightly slow. Good chord systems (Gmail, Linear) show the pending prefix. This one is invisible and silently drops.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/command-palette.tsx
- *Fix:* Show a small 'g…' pending-chord indicator (and the 500ms timeout) so I get feedback that the prefix is armed.

**523. ▫︎ Active tenant is a plain styled span indistinguishable from the switch buttons to a screen reader**
- *Surface:* Tenant switcher (header)
- *Why it's bad:* When I belong to 2+ businesses, the header shows the active tenant as a styled <span> and the others as buttons. To my screen reader the active one is just text reading the business name, with nothing saying 'this is your current business'. If two of my businesses have similar names I can't tell which one I'm actually operating on — and switching the wrong tenant means acting on the wrong live site.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/tenant-switcher.tsx (active <span> line 57-60; other tenants are <button> line 65)
- *Fix:* Add visually-hidden 'Current business:' before the active name, and ensure the switch buttons read like 'Switch to <name>' (the title is there but verify it's the accessible name).


## Navigation / IA / operator-leak (2)

**524. ⛔ No skip-to-content link anywhere — I tab through the whole sidebar on every single page**
- *Surface:* Global (every shell page)
- *Why it's bad:* I navigate by keyboard. The shell renders AppSidebar (5+ nav links), MobileSidebar, AppHeader (menu button, breadcrumb, tenant switcher, two kbd hints) BEFORE <main>. With no skip link, every page load forces me to Tab through ~10 chrome elements before I reach the actual content. Over a 6-month 'set and forget' workflow that's thousands of wasted keystrokes, and I never know how deep the chrome goes.
- *Grounding:* src/app/layout.tsx + src/app/(shell)/layout.tsx (ABSENCE: no skip link in either; grep for 'skip to content' returns nothing in src/)
- *Fix:* Add a visually-hidden, focus-visible 'Skip to main content' link as the first focusable element in body, give <main> id="main-content" tabIndex={-1}, and target it. ~10 lines in layout.tsx.

**525. ⚠️ There is no /changelog or activity-feed surface — 'what happened while I was gone' is scattered across 5 pages**
- *Surface:* global nav / changelog
- *Why it's bad:* My core need is one chronological digest of everything that happened. It doesn't exist. The nav (navigation.ts) is Today/Recommendations/Prompts/Changes/Settings. To reconstruct my 2 weeks I have to: check /changes for shipped edits, /recommendations for new queue items, /prompts for readings, /competitors for rival moves, and squint at the localStorage SinceLastVisit strip. There is no single timeline that interleaves 'shipped X on day 3, competitor Y added a page on day 6, new rec Z on day 9.' I'm forced to be the integrator — exactly the reconstruction work I hate.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no chronological cross-surface activity-feed/changelog route exists (navigation.ts has no such item; only per-domain pages)
- *Fix:* Build a single chronological Activity/Changelog feed (one route) that interleaves shipped changes, new recs, verified-live events, and competitor moves with dates, filterable by 'since last visit.'


## Pricing / cost / billing (1)

**526. ⛔ On Vercel the durable spend ledger is DEFAULT OFF — my spend may not be recorded at all**
- *Surface:* Supabase spend ledger (budget-ledger-supabase.ts)
- *Why it's bad:* The file-based ledger (cost-ledger.json) does not work on Vercel — ensureDataDir() bails when VERCEL===1 and writes go nowhere. The durable Supabase replacement (recordSpendDualWrite) is gated behind BEACON_BUDGET_LEDGER_DUAL_WRITE==='1' which is 'Default OFF.' So in the hosted product, with the flag unset, my actual LLM spend may never be persisted to a durable store — which means the daily/monthly caps that READ that store have nothing to read and silently fail-open (see prior finding). I'm told there are caps; in production they may be enforcing against an empty ledger.
- *Grounding:* src/lib/cost/budget-ledger-supabase.ts lines 11-14 (Default OFF), 120; src/lib/cost/budget.ts lines 136-142 (ensureDataDir returns early on VERCEL===1)
- *Fix:* Default the durable budget ledger ON in production, or make the cap fail-closed whenever the durable ledger is unavailable. Don't ship a spend cap whose backing store is off by default on the only platform customers run on.


## Performance / speed (1)

**527. ⚠️ Legacy /changes recomputes URL history + watcher + verdicts live on every load — multi-second wait to see my recap**
- *Surface:* /changes (legacy render path performance)
- *Why it's bad:* If I land on the legacy path, changes/page.tsx does a stack of live work per request: maybeRefreshUrlWatcher (line 189), buildUrlCitationHistory over a 60-day window (line 272), computeScorecard, plus per-row computeUrlVerdict over a dense series (lines 310-362). The comments even call out this used to 'walk the FULL canonical observation array on every /changes render — slow + heavy.' For Ritz-scale data that's a several-second blank wait just to find out what changed while I was away. Returning users want an instant recap, not a recompute.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Serve /changes from a precomputed snapshot (like the v2 persisted surface) instead of recomputing watcher + URL history + verdicts on every page load.


## Other / cross-cutting (10)

**528. ⛔ Per-URL page view — the heart of SEO work — is a permanent 'isn't ready yet' placeholder**
- *Surface:* /pages
- *Why it's bad:* Every SEO I know lives in a per-URL view: this page's impressions, clicks, position, queries, and the fixes for it. /pages renders a static card that says 'Pages isn't ready yet' and tells me to go look at Today/Recommendations/Changes instead. src/app/(shell)/pages/page.tsx is 134 lines of apology with zero data reads, and the doc comment defers the rebuild 'until customer 2 is on the calendar'. I connected GSC specifically to see page-level data and there is no page-level surface at all.
- *Grounding:* /Users/armeen/beacon/.claude/worktrees/objective-davinci-c81e70/src/app/(shell)/pages/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Build the bounded per-URL view the file's own comment describes: read gsc_page_signals (already computed in gsc-page-signals.ts) and render impressions/clicks/CTR/position + top queries + open recs per URL. The data already exists in Supabase; only the surface is missing.

**529. ⚠️ The 12-site switcher is a naked horizontal row of business-name text buttons — no dropdown, no search, no count**
- *Surface:* Tenant switcher (app header)
- *Why it's bad:* TenantSwitcher renders the active tenant as a pill plus EVERY other tenant as an inline `<form><button>` in a flex row (tenant-switcher.tsx lines 55-74). With 12 sites that's 11 plain-text buttons crammed into a 48px-tall header next to the breadcrumb and the ⌘K hint. No dropdown, no search box, no favicons, no truncation strategy, no 'which site am I on' affordance beyond a faint pill. It will wrap or overflow and is unusable at agency scale.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/tenant-switcher.tsx
- *Fix:* Replace the inline button row with a searchable dropdown/combobox showing all memberships, the active one checked, filterable by name — designed for 10+ tenants.

**530. ⚠️ The digest window is 'last 24h' — useless for a 14-day gap, and it can only reach the account owner**
- *Surface:* email digest (morning-digest composer)
- *Why it's bad:* Even in the fantasy where the digest reaches me, it's the wrong shape for someone returning after 2 weeks. selectDigestRows() and every receipts line (verifiedLastDay, pushedLastDay, firstCitations) use a hardcoded 24-hour window (morning-digest.ts lines 223-245). 13 of my 14 missing days are invisible. And src/lib/email/resend.ts line 43 defaults the sender to Resend's onboarding address which 'delivers ONLY to the account owner's address' — so a customer email literally cannot be delivered without extra config nobody set up.
- *Grounding:* src/domains/delivery/morning-digest.ts
- *Fix:* Add a 'period digest' mode that aggregates the full since-last-send window (e.g. 14 days) with per-day grouping, and require a verified per-tenant sender so customer emails can actually deliver.

**531. ⚠️ /changes has no 'new since last visit' marker — I cannot tell what's new vs what I already saw**
- *Surface:* /changes
- *Why it's bad:* This is supposed to be my timeline of what happened. But the v2 page (src/app/(shell)/changes/changes-v2-client.tsx) just renders the 24 most-recent cards 'newest first' with zero unseen/new highlighting — changes-v2-card.tsx has no 'new' badge, only a date formatter. After 14 days away every card looks identical whether it landed yesterday or before I left. I have to read every row and remember which I'd already seen. There's no read/unread state, no 'X new since June 1' divider, no per-card NEW pill.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/changes-v2-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Add a server-tracked last-seen line on /changes that draws a 'new since {date}' divider and marks unseen cards with a NEW pill.

**532. ⚠️ No in-app notification center, bell, or unread badge anywhere**
- *Surface:* global (notifications)
- *Why it's bad:* Returning users live and die by 'what needs my attention.' Beacon has no notification bell, no unread counter, no inbox — a grep across src/components and src/app finds no NotificationBell/unread/Inbox component. So nothing accumulates while I'm gone and nothing greets me with 'you have 9 items to review since June 1.' I have to proactively go fishing on each page. For a product sold as set-and-forget, the complete absence of a 'here's what piled up' surface is a fundamental gap.
- *Grounding:* ABSENCE: no notification-center/bell/unread component in src/components or src/app
- *Fix:* Add a lightweight notification center (server-backed, per-user) that accrues events while away and shows an unread count on return.

**533. ⚠️ Two different /changes pages exist with no warning — what I see depends on invisible env flags**
- *Surface:* /changes (legacy vs v2 split)
- *Why it's bad:* changes/page.tsx shouldUseChangesV2() (lines 81-87) routes me to a completely different layout based on BEACON_CHANGES_V2 env + ?v2=/?legacy= query params. The legacy table and the v2 timeline tell my 2-week story differently (different counters, different copy, different row caps). As a returning user I have no idea which one I'm looking at or that the other exists, and a ?legacy=1 link buried in the v2 footer can silently swap my whole view. I can't trust that 'Changes' is a stable place to reconstruct state when its very layout is flag-roulette.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Commit to one /changes layout for customers and remove the user-visible legacy/v2 divergence (keep any rollback flag operator-only and invisible).

**534. ▫︎ I come back to a 'possible duplicates' chore instead of a clean recap**
- *Surface:* /changes (dedupe banner)
- *Why it's bad:* On return, before I can even see what happened, /changes greets me with a dedupe banner (changes/page.tsx lines 531-548) — 'N possible duplicates in your changelog... Review once to clean the list,' linking to /changes/dedupe ('Archive the summaries so your changelog is clean'). I left for 2 weeks to NOT do janitorial work. The first thing the changes timeline does is hand me data-hygiene homework about CSV-vs-PDF entry matching, which is internal plumbing I never asked to think about. It pollutes the 'what happened' view with maintenance noise.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/page.tsx
- *Fix:* Auto-resolve high-confidence duplicates server-side (or hide the dedupe nag behind a Settings/data-hygiene area) so the Changes timeline stays focused on what actually happened.

**535. ▫︎ Most of my 2 weeks of changes will read 'Too early' because verdicts depend on polling that didn't run**
- *Surface:* /changes (verdict honesty for the away window)
- *Why it's bad:* computeUrlVerdict + the readyOn logic (changes/page.tsx lines 332-348, computeReadyOn) classify recent changes as 'too_early' and predict a 'Ready on [date]' based on a 7-day-ish bake window that assumes ongoing daily readings. With polls off during my absence, the post-change series is empty, so even a change shipped 13 days ago can still show 'too_early' / 'Beacon is collecting post-change readings.' I return expecting verdicts on a fortnight of work and instead get a wall of 'check back later' for changes that are objectively old.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/page.tsx
- *Fix:* When automated polling is off, the verdict copy should say 'verdict needs a fresh reading — Run one' rather than 'too early, Beacon is collecting readings,' which implies passive collection that isn't happening.

**536. ▫︎ My change's "What happened after" pill grades success on visibility, with the money line buried below as a soft sub-line**
- *Surface:* Changes Act 3 verdict pill
- *Why it's bad:* In the 5-act change brief, Act 3 leads with a visibility result pill + blurb (citations/verdict), and the outcome-attribution (sessions) line is appended at the bottom as a tiny gray sub-line with no bridge. The hierarchy tells me visibility is the headline and traffic is a footnote — exactly backwards for someone judging by results. And there's still no money in either.
- *Grounding:* src/app/(shell)/changes/[id]/change-detail-v2-client.tsx
- *Fix:* Reorder Act 3 so the business outcome (conversions/leads/sessions) is the headline and citation/visibility is supporting context, with a connecting sentence between them.

**537. ▫︎ Two keydown listeners run during the palette (global window + input onKeyDown); only the input handles arrows, but the global one still processes Escape and risks double-handling**
- *Surface:* Command palette (⌘K) — arrow keys
- *Why it's bad:* command-palette.tsx attaches a window keydown handler (line 93) AND the input has its own onKeyDown (line 176). The global handler early-returns for arrows when mode is set (line 54 'if (isInput || mode) return'), so navigation works — but Escape is handled in BOTH places (lines 48 and 140), and the dependency array re-binds the global listener on every mode change. It's fragile dual-ownership of keys in the surface I use most; any future edit to one path silently diverges from the other.
- *Grounding:* src/components/shell/command-palette.tsx
- *Fix:* Own all palette keys in one place (the input handler while open). Remove the duplicated Escape branch from the window handler when mode is active.
